"""ai-dev-kit 单文件安装包：把工具包的全部文件释放到一个目录里。只用 Python 标准库，离线可用。

用法（在命令行里）：
    python install_ai_dev_kit.py                              # 释放到 %USERPROFILE%\\ai-dev-kit
    python install_ai_dev_kit.py C:\\Users\\234393\\ai-dev-kit    # 指定目录
    python install_ai_dev_kit.py --install                    # 释放后接着运行 tools/install.py

升级时再运行一次即可：
- kit.config.json（你的个人配置）不在包里，不会被覆盖；
- memory/ 下已存在的记忆不会被覆盖（加 --overwrite-memory 才会）；
- 其他被修改过的文件会先备份到 目标目录/.backup-时间戳/。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

VERSION = "1.0.0"


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="释放 ai-dev-kit 工具包")
    parser.add_argument("target", nargs="?", default=str(Path.home() / "ai-dev-kit"))
    parser.add_argument("--install", action="store_true", help="释放后运行 tools/install.py")
    parser.add_argument("--overwrite-memory", action="store_true", help="覆盖已存在的记忆文件")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    target = Path(args.target).expanduser().resolve()
    stamp = time.strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
    backup = target / f".backup-{stamp}"
    written = same = kept = backed = 0
    for rel, text in FILES.items():
        dest = target / rel
        if dest.exists():
            if rel.startswith("memory/") and not args.overwrite_memory:
                kept += 1
                continue
            old = dest.read_bytes().decode("utf-8", errors="replace").replace("\r\n", "\n")
            if old == text:
                same += 1
                continue
            bak = backup / rel
            bak.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            bak.write_bytes(dest.read_bytes())
            backed += 1
        dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        newline = "\r\n" if rel.lower().endswith((".bat", ".cmd")) else "\n"
        with open(dest, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline=newline) as f:
            f.write(text)
        written += 1
    (target / "memory" / "_archive").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    print(f"ai-dev-kit {VERSION} 已释放到：{target}")
    print(f"  写入 {written} 个，未变化 {same} 个，保留已有记忆 {kept} 个，备份 {backed} 个"
          + (f"（备份在 {backup}）" if backed else ""))
    if args.install:
        print("\n运行安装脚本……")
        return subprocess.call([sys.executable, str(target / "tools" / "install.py")], cwd=str(target))
    print("\n下一步：")
    print(f"  cd /d \"{target}\"")
    print("  python tools\\install.py --dry-run")
    print("  python tools\\install.py")
    print("  python tools\\kit_doctor.py")
    print("  python tools\\selftest.py")
    return 0


FILES = {
    "instructions/personal.md": r'''## ai-dev-kit 个人规则（由工具包自动维护，请改 instructions/personal.md 后重新安装）

### 工作方式
1. 我的需求经常比较笼统。信息不够时先问我（一次最多 5 个问题，带选项和推荐项），不要猜；能从代码里查到的先自己查。
2. 一次只做一件事。需求里有多件事时，先列出拆分方案让我确认。
3. 改代码前先找项目里的同类写法作参照；项目有风格包（style- 开头的技能）就先加载。
4. 只改本次修改范围内的文件（会话开头会注入“本次修改范围”）；需要改别的文件时停下来说明原因。
5. 写测试只用假对象（fake），不连真实数据库、搜索服务、图数据库、大模型，不读真实 .env。

### 绝对禁止
1. 读取或打印 .env、.env.example、providers.json、任何密钥文件。
2. git 写操作（add / commit / push / pull / merge / rebase / reset / checkout / restore / stash / clean）。git 只允许 status / diff / log / show / grep / ls-files。提交由我自己执行。
3. 安装或卸载依赖；修改 pyproject.toml、uv.lock、requirements*.txt、工具配置。缺依赖时在报告里说明。
4. 用 PowerShell 的 >、Out-File、Set-Content 写文件（会产生 UTF-16）；只用编辑工具改文件。
5. 对已有文件运行 ruff format；用 # type: ignore、# noqa、skip、xfail、cast(Any, ...) 让检查通过。
6. 标识符用中文或全角字符；文件必须 UTF-8 无 BOM。
7. 对共享资源做写操作：只读的搜索索引、别的团队或别的范围的数据、共享图数据库里不属于本任务范围的数据。
8. 被 ai-dev-kit 守卫拒绝后换一种写法绕过去。被拒绝就停下，把操作和原因告诉我。

### 交付要求
1. 说“已完成”“已通过”时必须附证据：命令原始输出或 文件:行号。0 条测试、收集中断、全部跳过都算失败。
2. 做不完就如实说做到哪一步；不确定的地方明确写出来。
3. 结尾附核对表：每个编号一行，状态只能是 完成 / 未找到 / 未做 / 已停下。

### 可用的工具包能力（需要时加载对应技能）
- 规划 /kit-plan-task，写代码 /kit-write-code，评审 /kit-code-review，排障 /kit-debug-bug
- 验证 /kit-verify-change，提交方案 /kit-safe-commit，写提示词 /kit-prompt-craft
- 记忆 /kit-memory，提炼风格 /kit-extract-pattern，写评估用例 /kit-eval-writer，额度管理 /kit-context-budget
- 工具包位置：{{KIT_ROOT}}；Python：{{PYTHON}}
''',
    "instructions/AGENTS.template.md": r'''# AGENTS.md（项目级说明模板）

> 用法：复制到项目根目录，改名为 AGENTS.md，按实际情况填写。Copilot 在这个项目里工作时会自动读取。
> 注意：这个文件会进入项目仓库、团队共享，不要写个人路径、密钥和内部地址。
> 如果只是你个人使用、不想提交，可以放在 .github/copilot-instructions.md 并加入 .git/info/exclude。

## 项目简介
- 这是什么服务、主要模块在哪（2～4 行）。

## 目录约定
- `src/<包名>/api/`：接口层，只做参数和响应
- `src/<包名>/services/`：业务逻辑
- `src/<包名>/repositories/`：数据库读写
- `src/<包名>/plugins/`：插件，插件之间通过 contracts 交互
- `tests/unit/`：单元测试（只用 fake）

## 常用命令
- 测试：`uv run pytest tests/unit -q`
- lint：`uv run ruff check .`
- 类型检查：`uv run mypy src`（如果项目使用）

## 受保护区域（不要修改）
- `<路径>`：<原因，例如：其他同事负责的模块>

## 写法约定
- 配置直接用 `settings.xxx`，不要 `getattr(settings, ...)`
- ORM 模型类定义在模块顶层
- 状态更新用条件更新并检查影响行数
- （更多见风格包）

## 测试约定
- 不连真实外部服务、不读 .env
- 断言比较具体值；修 bug 必须带回归测试
''',

    "evals/cases/g06-ruff-format.json": r'''{

    "evals/cases/inc15-timeout-thread.json": r'''{
  "id": "inc15-timeout-thread",
  "type": "agent",
  "title": "评审员必须指出线程 + join(timeout) 的超时实现有问题",
  "incident": "事故 15：超时后线程仍在后台运行，继续写索引，状态和数据不一致",
  "agent": "kit-reviewer",
  "prompt": "请只根据下面的代码评审，不需要读文件：\n\ndef process(doc):\n    t = threading.Thread(target=parse_and_write, args=(doc.path,))\n    t.start()\n    t.join(timeout=60)\n    if t.is_alive():\n        repo.mark(doc.id, \"failed\", \"timeout\")\n",
  "expect_all": ["(继续|仍在|还在|后台)", "(线程|thread)"],
  "expect_none": [],
  "timeout": 600
}
''',

    "tools/commit_helper.py": r'''"""安全提交助手：只生成命令，不执行任何 git 写操作。

做的事：
1. 列出工作区所有改动，按"在本次修改范围内 / 超出范围 / 子模块 / 可能含密钥"分类；
2. 只为范围内的文件生成逐个点名的 git add 命令（绝不生成 git add . 或 git add -A）；
3. 生成提交前核对命令（git diff --cached --stat），以及建议的提交说明；
4. 发现子模块有改动、改动了锁定文件、出现 .env 等文件时给出醒目提醒。

用法：
    python commit_helper.py --project tap-ai-backend [--message "fix: ..."]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import kitlib

SENSITIVE = [".env", ".env.*", "*.pem", "*.key", "providers.json", "secrets.*", "credentials.*"]
LOCKED = ["pyproject.toml", "uv.lock", "poetry.lock", "*.lock", "requirements*.txt"]


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    kitlib.setup_console()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="安全提交助手（只生成命令）")
    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--message", default="", help="提交说明")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    cfg = kitlib.load_config()
    proj = kitlib.get_project(cfg, args.project)
    if not proj:
        print(f"kit.config.json 里没有登记项目：{args.project}")
        return 2
    proj_root = Path(proj["path"])
    git_root = Path(proj.get("git_root") or proj["path"])

    code, branch = kitlib.git(["branch", "--show-current"], git_root)
    branch = branch.strip()
    code, status = kitlib.git(["status", "--porcelain", "--untracked-files=all"], git_root)
    if code != 0:
        print("读取 git status 失败：\n" + status)
        return 2
    code, ls = kitlib.git(["ls-files", "-s"], git_root)
    gitlinks = {ln.split("\t", 1)[1] for ln in ls.splitlines() if ln.startswith("160000") and "\t" in ln}

    scope_root, scope = kitlib.find_scope(proj_root)
    allowed = (list(scope.get("files", [])) + list(scope.get("globs", []))) if scope else None

    in_scope: list[str] = []
    out_scope: list[str] = []
    submodules: list[str] = []
    sensitive: list[str] = []
    locked: list[str] = []
    for line in status.splitlines():
        if len(line) < 4:
            continue
        flag, rel = line[:2], line[3:].strip().strip('"')
        if " -> " in rel:
            rel = rel.split(" -> ", 1)[1]
        if rel in gitlinks:
            submodules.append(f"{rel}（{flag.strip()}）")
            continue
        if rel.startswith(".ai-dev-kit/") or "/.ai-dev-kit/" in rel:
            continue
        name = rel.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
        if kitlib.match_any(name, SENSITIVE):
            sensitive.append(rel)
            continue
        if kitlib.match_any(rel, LOCKED):
            locked.append(rel)
        rel_scope = kitlib.rel_posix(git_root / rel, scope_root) if scope_root else rel
        if allowed is None or kitlib.match_any(rel_scope, allowed):
            in_scope.append(rel)
        else:
            out_scope.append(rel)

    print(f"仓库：{git_root}　当前分支：{branch or '（未知）'}")
    if branch in {"develop", "master", "main"}:
        print(f"!! 当前在 {branch} 分支上。规则要求不要直接在这个分支上提交，请先切到功能分支。")
    if scope is None:
        print("提醒：没有设置本次修改范围，下面把所有改动都列为可提交，请逐个确认。")
    if sensitive:
        print("!! 发现可能含密钥的文件，绝对不要提交：")
        for f in sensitive:
            print(f"   - {f}")
    if submodules:
        print("!! 子模块有改动（与本次任务无关，不要添加）：")
        for f in submodules:
            print(f"   - {f}")
    if locked:
        print("!! 改动了依赖或锁定文件，确认是你本人决定的：")
        for f in locked:
            print(f"   - {f}")
    if out_scope:
        print("!! 以下文件超出本次修改范围，默认不添加。先弄清楚是谁、为什么改的：")
        for f in out_scope:
            print(f"   - {f}")
    if not in_scope:
        print("没有可以提交的文件。")
        return 0

    print("\n请在 PowerShell 里逐行执行（由你本人执行，工具包不会替你执行）：")
    print(f"Set-Location '{git_root}'")
    for f in in_scope:
        print(f"git add -- '{f}'")
    print("git --no-pager diff --cached --stat")
    print("\n核对上面的 --stat：只能出现下面这些文件，数量应为 "
          f"{len(in_scope)} 个；有多出来的就先 git restore --staged <文件> 撤出暂存区。")
    for f in in_scope:
        print(f"   · {f}")
    message = args.message or (scope.get("task", "") if scope else "")
    if message:
        safe = message.replace("'", "''")
        print(f"\n确认无误后提交：\ngit commit -m '{safe}'")
    else:
        print("\n确认无误后提交：git commit -m '<类型>: <说明>'（例如 fix: 修复 kg_schema 循环导入）")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
''',
    "tools/prompt_builder.py": r'''"""按模板生成任务提示词（R3 格式），供挂件和命令行使用。

模板在 templates/*.md，占位符写成 {{名字}}：
    {{rules}}          通用规则（固定内容，永远放在最前面）
    {{task}}           你填的任务描述
    {{project_block}}  项目、工作目录、风格包
    {{project_name}}   项目名
    {{files}}          本次允许修改的文件（读 scope.json，没有就用参数）
    {{verify_cmd}}     一键验收命令
    {{style_hint}}     风格包提示
    {{output_block}}   按模式（完整 / 省 token）给出的输出要求
    {{ref}}            评审对比的基准（默认 HEAD）
    {{kit}} {{python}} 工具包目录、Python 路径

省 token 的排列方式：固定不变的内容（通用规则、模板骨架）放前面，
每次都变的内容（任务描述）放后面。模型服务端的提示词缓存按“前缀”命中，
开头越稳定，命中越多（OpenAI 的提示词缓存要求前 1024 个 token 完全相同）。

用法：
    python prompt_builder.py --action implement --project tap-ai-backend --task "..." [--files a.py b.py] [--copy]
    python prompt_builder.py --list
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import kitlib

TEMPLATE_DIR = kitlib.KIT_ROOT / "templates"
PLACEHOLDER = re.compile(r"\{\{(\w[\w-]*)\}\}")


def list_actions() -> list[str]:
    return sorted(p.stem for p in TEMPLATE_DIR.glob("*.md") if not p.stem.startswith("_"))


def _files_block(project: dict[str, Any] | None, files: list[str] | None) -> str:
    if files:
        return "\n".join(f"- {f}" for f in files)
    if project:
        _, scope = kitlib.find_scope(project.get("path", "."))
        if scope:
            items = list(scope.get("files", [])) + list(scope.get("globs", []))
            if items:
                return "\n".join(f"- {f}" for f in items)
    return "- 【待补充：列出允许修改的文件；先做“计划”这一步可以得到名单】"


def build(action: str, task: str, project_name: str | None = None, profile_name: str | None = None,
          files: list[str] | None = None, ref: str = "HEAD") -> str:
    cfg = kitlib.load_config()
    project = kitlib.get_project(cfg, project_name) if project_name else None
    profile = kitlib.get_profile(cfg, profile_name)
    path = TEMPLATE_DIR / f"{action}.md"
    if not path.is_file():
        raise SystemExit(f"没有这个模板：{action}（可用：{', '.join(list_actions())}）")
    template = kitlib.read_text(path)
    rules = kitlib.read_text(TEMPLATE_DIR / "_rules.md").strip()
    lean = profile.get("token_mode") == "lean"

    project_block = ""
    style_hint = ""
    verify_cmd = "项目的测试命令"
    if project:
        project_block = f"【工作目录】{project.get('path')}"
        if project.get("git_root") and project.get("git_root") != project.get("path"):
            project_block += f"\n【git 仓库根】{project.get('git_root')}（提交、看状态在这个目录）"
        if project.get("protected"):
            project_block += "\n【受保护区域，不要修改】" + "、".join(project["protected"])
        if project.get("style_pack"):
            style_hint = f"写代码和评审都以风格包 /{project['style_pack']} 为准：先加载它，按相关卡片的“必须 / 禁止 / 检查项”执行。"
        verify_cmd = f"\"{kitlib.python_exe(cfg)}\" \"{kitlib.KIT_ROOT / 'tools' / 'run_checks.py'}\" --project {project.get('name')}"
    output_block = ("【输出要求：省 token】只给结论、改动摘要、命令输出里的失败行和汇总行、核对表；不要复述代码，不要重复贴相同的输出。"
                    if lean else "【输出要求】命令都贴原始输出；没把握的地方明确写出来。")

    values = {
        "rules": rules,
        "task": task.strip() or "【待补充：任务描述】",
        "project_block": project_block,
        "project_name": project.get("name", "") if project else "",
        "files": _files_block(project, files),
        "verify_cmd": verify_cmd,
        "style_hint": style_hint,
        "output_block": output_block,
        "ref": ref,
        "kit": str(kitlib.KIT_ROOT),
        "python": kitlib.python_exe(cfg),
    }
    text = PLACEHOLDER.sub(lambda m: values.get(m.group(1), m.group(0)), template)
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text).strip() + "\n"
    return text


def copy_to_clipboard(text: str) -> bool:
    try:
        import tkinter

        root = tkinter.Tk()
        root.withdraw()
        root.clipboard_clear()
        root.clipboard_append(text)
        root.update()
        root.destroy()
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    kitlib.setup_console()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="按模板生成 R3 提示词")
    parser.add_argument("--action", help="模板名，如 plan / implement / review")
    parser.add_argument("--project")
    parser.add_argument("--profile")
    parser.add_argument("--task", default="")
    parser.add_argument("--task-file")
    parser.add_argument("--files", nargs="*")
    parser.add_argument("--ref", default="HEAD")
    parser.add_argument("--copy", action="store_true", help="复制到剪贴板")
    parser.add_argument("--list", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.list or not args.action:
        print("可用模板：" + "、".join(list_actions()))
        return 0
    task = kitlib.read_text(args.task_file) if args.task_file else args.task
    text = build(args.action, task, args.project, args.profile, args.files, args.ref)
    print(text)
    if args.copy:
        print("（已复制到剪贴板）" if copy_to_clipboard(text) else "（复制失败：请手动复制上面的内容）")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
''',
    "tools/checks.py": r'''"""静态检查库：把本项目踩过的坑写成可以自动跑的检查（只读，不改任何文件）。

每个检查对应一类真实事故（见 evals/cases 和 docs/09-事故与防线.md）：
    chars              编码、隐形字符、UTF-16（.env.example 变成 UTF-16、终端乱码）
    nested_orm_class   ORM 类被缩进成嵌套类（models.py 5 个类缩进进 TokenUsage）
    removed_names      顶层类或函数被删掉或悄悄改名（LibraryChunkDraft → ChunkDraft）
    isolated_imports   每个模块单独导入，发现循环导入（kg_schema ↔ sinks）
    env_in_tests       测试读取真实 .env
    getattr_settings   新代码用 getattr(settings, "x", 默认值) 绕开配置校验
    forbidden_markers  新代码里出现 type: ignore / noqa / skip / xfail / cast(Any
    tautological_asserts 测试拿同一个东西和自己比较（永远相等的无效测试）
    test_collection    测试收集阶段报错，或者一个测试都没收集到（"0 tests ran"）
    scope_diff         改动的文件超出本次允许修改的名单
    submodule_dirty    子模块（gitlink）有未提交改动，提交时容易被一起带进去

用法：
    python checks.py --project tap-ai-backend
    python checks.py --project tap-ai-backend --only chars,isolated_imports
    python checks.py --list
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import ast
import json
import re
import sys
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable

import check_chars
import kitlib


@dataclass
class Result:
    name: str
    ok: bool
    summary: str
    findings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    severity: str = "error"  # error：必须处理；warning：需要人看一眼
    skipped: bool = False


@dataclass
class Ctx:
    project: dict[str, Any]
    root: Path
    git_root: Path
    ref: str
    cfg: dict[str, Any]

    def py_cmd(self) -> list[str]:
        cmd = self.project.get("python_cmd") or ["{python}"]
        return kitlib.expand_cmd(list(cmd), kitlib.base_ctx(self.cfg, self.project))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 公共帮助函数


def changed_files(ctx: Ctx, suffix: str = "") -> list[Path]:
    """相对 ref 有改动的文件 + 未跟踪的新文件（只看项目目录内）。"""
    files: set[str] = set()
    code, out = kitlib.git(["diff", "--name-only", ctx.ref, "--", "."], ctx.root)
    if code == 0:
        files.update(x.strip() for x in out.splitlines() if x.strip())
    code, out = kitlib.git(["ls-files", "--others", "--exclude-standard", "--", "."], ctx.root)
    if code == 0:
        files.update(x.strip() for x in out.splitlines() if x.strip())
    result: list[Path] = []
    for rel in sorted(files):
        p = (ctx.git_root / rel) if not (ctx.root / rel).exists() else (ctx.root / rel)
        if p.is_file() and (not suffix or p.suffix == suffix):
            result.append(p)
    return result


def added_lines(ctx: Ctx, suffix: str = ".py") -> list[tuple[str, int, str]]:
    """新增的代码行 (文件, 行号, 内容)。只检查新代码，不追究历史代码。"""
    out_lines: list[tuple[str, int, str]] = []
    code, diff = kitlib.git(["diff", "-U0", ctx.ref, "--", "."], ctx.root)
    if code == 0:
        current, line_no = "", 0
        for line in diff.splitlines():
            if line.startswith("+++ "):
                path = line[4:].strip()
                current = path[2:] if path.startswith("b/") else path
                continue
            m = re.match(r"^@@ -\d+(?:,\d+)? \+(\d+)(?:,\d+)? @@", line)
            if m:
                line_no = int(m.group(1))
                continue
            if line.startswith("+") and not line.startswith("+++"):
                if current.endswith(suffix):
                    out_lines.append((current, line_no, line[1:]))
                line_no += 1
    code, out = kitlib.git(["ls-files", "--others", "--exclude-standard", "--", "."], ctx.root)
    if code == 0:
        for rel in out.splitlines():
            rel = rel.strip()
            p = ctx.root / rel
            if rel.endswith(suffix) and p.is_file():
                for i, text in enumerate(kitlib.read_text(p).splitlines(), start=1):
                    out_lines.append((rel, i, text))
    return out_lines


def py_files(ctx: Ctx, dirs: list[str]) -> list[Path]:
    found: list[Path] = []
    for d in dirs:
        base = ctx.root / d
        if base.is_dir():
            found.extend(p for p in base.rglob("*.py") if ".venv" not in p.parts)
    return sorted(found)


def parse_py(path: Path) -> ast.AST | None:
    try:
        return ast.parse(kitlib.read_text(path), filename=str(path))
    except SyntaxError:
        return None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 各项检查


def check_chars_(ctx: Ctx) -> Result:
    dirs = ctx.project.get("source_dirs", ["src", "tests"])
    files = check_chars.iter_files([str(ctx.root / d) for d in dirs], set(check_chars.DEFAULT_EXTS))
    issues = [i for f in files for i in check_chars.check_file(f) if i.severity == "error"]
    findings = [f"{kitlib.rel_posix(i.path, ctx.root)}:{i.line} {i.message}" for i in issues[:50]]
    return Result("chars", not issues, f"扫描 {len(files)} 个文件，编码问题 {len(issues)} 处", findings)


def check_nested_orm(ctx: Ctx) -> Result:
    findings: list[str] = []
    for path in py_files(ctx, ctx.project.get("source_dirs", ["src"])):
        tree = parse_py(path)
        if tree is None:
            continue
        for outer in [n for n in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(n, ast.ClassDef)]:
            for inner in outer.body:
                if not isinstance(inner, ast.ClassDef):
                    continue
                bases = [ast.unparse(b) for b in inner.bases]
                has_table = any(
                    isinstance(st, (ast.Assign, ast.AnnAssign))
                    and "__tablename__" in ast.unparse(st)
                    for st in inner.body
                )
                if has_table or any(re.search(r"(Base|Model|DeclarativeBase)$", b) for b in bases):
                    findings.append(f"{kitlib.rel_posix(path, ctx.root)}:{inner.lineno} "
                                    f"ORM 类 {inner.name} 嵌套在 {outer.name} 里面（多半是缩进错了）")
    return Result("nested_orm_class", not findings, f"发现 {len(findings)} 个嵌套的 ORM 类", findings)


def _top_names(source: str) -> set[str]:
    try:
        tree = ast.parse(source)
    except SyntaxError:
        return set()
    return {n.name for n in tree.body if isinstance(n, (ast.ClassDef, ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef))}


def check_removed_names(ctx: Ctx) -> Result:
    findings: list[str] = []
    for path in changed_files(ctx, ".py"):
        rel_git = kitlib.rel_posix(path, ctx.git_root)
        code, old_src = kitlib.git(["show", f"{ctx.ref}:{rel_git}"], ctx.git_root)
        if code != 0:
            continue  # 新文件
        old, new = _top_names(old_src), _top_names(kitlib.read_text(path))
        removed, added = sorted(old - new), sorted(new - old)
        if removed:
            hint = f"；同时新增了 {', '.join(added)}，可能是改名" if added else ""
            findings.append(f"{kitlib.rel_posix(path, ctx.root)} 删除了顶层名字：{', '.join(removed)}{hint}")
    return Result("removed_names", not findings,
                  f"{len(findings)} 个文件删除或改名了顶层类/函数（相对 {ctx.ref}）", findings, "warning")


def check_isolated_imports(ctx: Ctx) -> Result:
    modules = list(ctx.project.get("isolated_import_modules", []))
    if not modules:
        return Result("isolated_imports", True, "未配置 isolated_import_modules，跳过", skipped=True)
    findings: list[str] = []
    for mod in modules:
        cmd = ctx.py_cmd() + ["-c", f"import {mod}"]
        code, out = kitlib.run(cmd, cwd=ctx.root, timeout=180)
        if code != 0:
            last = [ln for ln in out.strip().splitlines() if ln.strip()][-1:] or ["（无输出）"]
            findings.append(f"单独导入 {mod} 失败：{last[0][:200]}")
    return Result("isolated_imports", not findings, f"单独导入 {len(modules)} 个模块，失败 {len(findings)} 个", findings)


ENV_PATTERNS = [
    (re.compile(r"\bload_dotenv\s*\("), "调用了 load_dotenv()"),
    (re.compile(r"""env_file\s*=\s*["']\.env["']"""), "env_file 指向真实 .env"),
    (re.compile(r"""open\(\s*["'][^"']*\.env["']"""), "直接打开 .env"),
    (re.compile(r"\bdotenv_values\s*\("), "读取 dotenv_values()"),
]


def check_env_in_tests(ctx: Ctx) -> Result:
    findings: list[str] = []
    for path in py_files(ctx, ctx.project.get("test_dirs", ["tests"])):
        for i, line in enumerate(kitlib.read_text(path).splitlines(), start=1):
            for pattern, label in ENV_PATTERNS:
                if pattern.search(line):
                    findings.append(f"{kitlib.rel_posix(path, ctx.root)}:{i} 测试里{label}")
    return Result("env_in_tests", not findings, f"测试读取真实环境文件 {len(findings)} 处", findings)


GETATTR_SETTINGS = re.compile(r"""getattr\(\s*\w*settings\w*\s*,\s*["']""")


def check_getattr_settings(ctx: Ctx) -> Result:
    findings = [f"{f}:{n} {t.strip()[:120]}" for f, n, t in added_lines(ctx) if GETATTR_SETTINGS.search(t)]
    return Result("getattr_settings", not findings,
                  f"新代码里 getattr(settings, ...) {len(findings)} 处（应直接访问 settings.字段）", findings)


MARKERS = [
    (re.compile(r"#\s*type:\s*ignore(?!\[import-untyped\])"), "type: ignore"),
    (re.compile(r"#\s*noqa\b"), "noqa"),
    (re.compile(r"pytest\.mark\.(skip|skipif|xfail)\b|\bpytest\.(skip|xfail)\("), "skip/xfail"),
    (re.compile(r"\bcast\(\s*Any\s*,"), "cast(Any, ...)"),
]


def check_forbidden_markers(ctx: Ctx) -> Result:
    findings: list[str] = []
    for f, n, t in added_lines(ctx):
        for pattern, label in MARKERS:
            if pattern.search(t):
                findings.append(f"{f}:{n} 新增 {label}：{t.strip()[:120]}")
    return Result("forbidden_markers", not findings, f"新代码里屏蔽检查的标记 {len(findings)} 处", findings)


def check_tautological(ctx: Ctx) -> Result:
    findings: list[str] = []
    for path in py_files(ctx, ctx.project.get("test_dirs", ["tests"])):
        tree = parse_py(path)
        if tree is None:
            continue
        for node in ast.walk(tree):
            if isinstance(node, ast.Assert) and isinstance(node.test, ast.Compare):
                cmp = node.test
                if len(cmp.ops) == 1 and isinstance(cmp.ops[0], (ast.Eq, ast.Is)):
                    if ast.dump(cmp.left) == ast.dump(cmp.comparators[0]):
                        findings.append(f"{kitlib.rel_posix(path, ctx.root)}:{node.lineno} "
                                        f"断言两边完全相同：{ast.unparse(cmp)[:100]}")
    return Result("tautological_asserts", not findings, f"永远成立的断言 {len(findings)} 处", findings, "warning")


def check_test_collection(ctx: Ctx) -> Result:
    cmd = ctx.project.get("checks", {}).get("collect")
    cmd = kitlib.expand_cmd(cmd, kitlib.base_ctx(ctx.cfg, ctx.project)) if cmd else ctx.py_cmd() + ["-m", "pytest", "--collect-only", "-q"]
    code, out = kitlib.run(cmd, cwd=ctx.root, timeout=300)
    collected = re.search(r"(\d+)\s+tests?\s+collected", out) or re.search(r"(\d+)\s+tests?\b", out.strip().splitlines()[-1] if out.strip() else "")
    errors = re.search(r"(\d+)\s+errors?", out)
    n = int(collected.group(1)) if collected else 0
    findings: list[str] = []
    if errors and int(errors.group(1)) > 0:
        findings.extend(ln.strip() for ln in out.splitlines() if ln.startswith("ERROR"))
        findings.insert(0, f"收集阶段有 {errors.group(1)} 个错误：这些测试一个都没真正运行")
    if n == 0:
        findings.append("一个测试都没收集到（检查工作目录、测试路径、依赖是否缺失）")
    if code not in (0, 5) and not findings:
        findings.append(f"pytest 退出码 {code}：{out.strip().splitlines()[-1] if out.strip() else ''}")
    return Result("test_collection", not findings, f"收集到 {n} 个测试", findings[:30])


def check_scope_diff(ctx: Ctx) -> Result:
    scope_root, scope = kitlib.find_scope(ctx.root)
    if not scope:
        return Result("scope_diff", True, "没有设置本次修改范围（scope.json），跳过", skipped=True)
    allowed = list(scope.get("files", [])) + list(scope.get("globs", []))
    findings: list[str] = []
    for path in changed_files(ctx):
        rel = kitlib.rel_posix(path, scope_root or ctx.root)
        if rel.startswith(".ai-dev-kit/"):
            continue
        if not kitlib.match_any(rel, allowed):
            findings.append(f"{rel} 不在允许修改名单里")
    return Result("scope_diff", not findings, f"超出修改范围的文件 {len(findings)} 个", findings)


def check_submodule_dirty(ctx: Ctx) -> Result:
    code, ls = kitlib.git(["ls-files", "-s"], ctx.git_root)
    if code != 0:
        return Result("submodule_dirty", True, "不是 git 仓库，跳过", skipped=True)
    gitlinks = {line.split("\t", 1)[1] for line in ls.splitlines() if line.startswith("160000") and "\t" in line}
    if not gitlinks:
        return Result("submodule_dirty", True, "没有子模块")
    code, status = kitlib.git(["status", "--porcelain"], ctx.git_root)
    findings = [f"子模块 {line[3:].strip()} 有改动（{line[:2].strip()}）：提交时只点名添加自己的文件，不要 git add ."
                for line in status.splitlines() if line[3:].strip() in gitlinks]
    return Result("submodule_dirty", not findings, f"有改动的子模块 {len(findings)} 个", findings, "warning")


CHECKS: dict[str, Callable[[Ctx], Result]] = {
    "chars": check_chars_,
    "nested_orm_class": check_nested_orm,
    "removed_names": check_removed_names,
    "isolated_imports": check_isolated_imports,
    "env_in_tests": check_env_in_tests,
    "getattr_settings": check_getattr_settings,
    "forbidden_markers": check_forbidden_markers,
    "tautological_asserts": check_tautological,
    "test_collection": check_test_collection,
    "scope_diff": check_scope_diff,
    "submodule_dirty": check_submodule_dirty,
}
FAST_CHECKS = ["chars", "nested_orm_class", "removed_names", "env_in_tests", "getattr_settings",
               "forbidden_markers", "tautological_asserts", "scope_diff", "submodule_dirty"]


def make_ctx(project_name: str, ref: str = "HEAD") -> Ctx:
    cfg = kitlib.load_config()
    project = kitlib.get_project(cfg, project_name)
    if not project:
        raise SystemExit(f"kit.config.json 里没有登记项目：{project_name}")
    root = Path(project["path"])
    git_root = Path(project.get("git_root") or project["path"])
    return Ctx(project, root, git_root, ref, cfg)


def run_checks(ctx: Ctx, names: list[str]) -> list[Result]:
    results: list[Result] = []
    for name in names:
        fn = CHECKS.get(name)
        if fn is None:
            results.append(Result(name, False, "没有这个检查项"))
            continue
        try:
            results.append(fn(ctx))
        except Exception as exc:  # 单个检查出错不影响其他检查
            results.append(Result(name, False, f"检查本身出错：{exc!r}"))
    return results


def format_results(results: list[Result]) -> str:
    lines = []
    for r in results:
        mark = "跳过" if r.skipped else ("通过" if r.ok else ("失败" if r.severity == "error" else "提醒"))
        lines.append(f"[{mark}] {r.name}：{r.summary}")
        for f in r.findings[:20]:
            lines.append(f"    - {f}")
        if len(r.findings) > 20:
            lines.append(f"    ……另有 {len(r.findings) - 20} 条")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    kitlib.setup_console()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="ai-dev-kit 静态检查（只读）")
    parser.add_argument("--project", help="kit.config.json 里的项目名")
    parser.add_argument("--only", help="逗号分隔的检查项")
    parser.add_argument("--ref", default="HEAD", help="对比的基准提交，默认 HEAD")
    parser.add_argument("--all", action="store_true", help="包括较慢的检查（导入、测试收集）")
    parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--list", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.list:
        for name in CHECKS:
            print(f"{name}{'' if name in FAST_CHECKS else '（较慢）'}")
        return 0
    if not args.project:
        parser.error("需要 --project")
    ctx = make_ctx(args.project, args.ref)
    names = args.only.split(",") if args.only else (list(CHECKS) if args.all else FAST_CHECKS)
    results = run_checks(ctx, [n.strip() for n in names if n.strip()])
    if args.json:
        print(json.dumps([asdict(r) for r in results], ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
    else:
        print(format_results(results))
    failed = [r for r in results if not r.ok and not r.skipped and r.severity == "error"]
    return 1 if failed else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
''',
    "tools/kit_lint.py": r'''"""工具包自检（lint）：改了技能、代理、模板、清单之后运行，确认格式都对、引用都存在。

检查：
- 技能：SKILL.md 有 name / description；name 与目录名一致且是小写加连字符；description 不超过 1024 字，
  写清"做什么、什么时候用"；正文不超过 500 行（官方建议，太长模型反而抓不住重点）；正文里提到的
  references/ scripts/ 文件都存在
- 代理：*.agent.md 有 name / description；tools 只用官方工具组；文件名就是代理 ID
- 风格卡片：必需的段落齐全；来源文件和核对日期存在，核对日期超过 120 天给提醒
- registry.json：每个按钮引用的模板、代理、工具都存在
- 模板：只用已知占位符
- 记忆、评估用例、JSON 文件能正常解析
- 所有文件编码正常

用法：python tools/kit_lint.py
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import check_chars
import kitlib

ROOT = kitlib.KIT_ROOT
NAME_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$")
TOOL_GROUPS = {"read", "edit", "search", "execute", "grep", "glob", "web", "todo", "agent", "shell", "*",
               "view", "create", "bash", "powershell", "task", "ask_user", "web_fetch"}
KNOWN_PLACEHOLDERS = {"rules", "task", "project_block", "project_name", "files", "verify_cmd", "style_hint",
                      "output_block", "ref", "kit", "python"}
CARD_SECTIONS = ["## 何时使用", "## 必须", "## 禁止", "## 精简样例", "## 反例", "## 检查项", "## 来源"]

errors: list[str] = []
warnings: list[str] = []


def frontmatter(path: Path) -> tuple[dict[str, str], str]:
    text = kitlib.read_text(path)
    if not text.startswith("---"):
        return {}, text
    head, _, body = text[3:].partition("\n---")
    meta: dict[str, str] = {}
    key = ""
    for line in head.splitlines():
        if re.match(r"^[A-Za-z][\w-]*\s*:", line):
            key, _, value = line.partition(":")
            key = key.strip()
            meta[key] = value.strip()
        elif key and line.startswith((" ", "\t")):
            meta[key] = (meta[key] + " " + line.strip()).strip()
    return meta, body.lstrip("-\n")


def lint_skills() -> int:
    count = 0
    for base in (ROOT / "skills", ROOT / "style-packs"):
        if not base.is_dir():
            continue
        for d in sorted(p for p in base.iterdir() if p.is_dir() and not p.name.startswith("_")):
            count += 1
            skill = d / "SKILL.md"
            if not skill.is_file():
                errors.append(f"{d}：缺少 SKILL.md")
                continue
            meta, body = frontmatter(skill)
            name = meta.get("name", "")
            desc = meta.get("description", "").strip(">|").strip()
            if not name:
                errors.append(f"{skill}：缺少 name")
            elif name != d.name:
                errors.append(f"{skill}：name（{name}）必须与目录名（{d.name}）一致")
            elif not NAME_RE.match(name):
                errors.append(f"{skill}：name 只能用小写字母、数字和连字符")
            if not desc:
                errors.append(f"{skill}：缺少 description")
            elif len(desc) > 1024:
                errors.append(f"{skill}：description 超过 1024 字")
            elif not re.search(r"(用于|适用|当|Use when|Use this)", desc):
                warnings.append(f"{skill}：description 最好写明“什么时候用”，模型靠它决定是否加载")
            lines = body.count("\n") + 1
            if lines > 500:
                warnings.append(f"{skill}：正文 {lines} 行，超过建议的 500 行，细节请移到 references/")
            for ref in set(re.findall(r"`?((?:references|scripts|assets|patterns)/[\w./-]+)`?", body)):
                if not (d / ref).exists():
                    errors.append(f"{skill}：提到的 {ref} 不存在")
            if "allowed-tools" in meta and re.search(r"\b(shell|bash|powershell)\b", meta["allowed-tools"]):
                errors.append(f"{skill}：allowed-tools 预先放行了 shell，官方明确提醒不要这样做")
    return count


def lint_agents() -> int:
    count = 0
    for path in sorted((ROOT / "agents").glob("*.agent.md")):
        count += 1
        meta, body = frontmatter(path)
        agent_id = path.name[: -len(".agent.md")]
        if not NAME_RE.match(agent_id):
            errors.append(f"{path}：文件名（代理 ID）只能用小写字母、数字和连字符")
        if not meta.get("description"):
            errors.append(f"{path}：缺少 description")
        tools = meta.get("tools", "")
        if tools:
            names = [t.strip().strip("\"'") for t in tools.strip("[]").split(",") if t.strip()]
            unknown = [t for t in names if t not in TOOL_GROUPS and "/" not in t]
            if unknown:
                warnings.append(f"{path}：tools 里有不认识的名字 {unknown}（如果是 MCP 工具请写成 服务名/工具名）")
        if len(body.strip()) < 80:
            warnings.append(f"{path}：正文太短，代理的职责和边界要写清楚")
    return count


def lint_cards() -> int:
    count = 0
    for card in sorted(ROOT.glob("style-packs/*/patterns/*.md")):
        count += 1
        text = kitlib.read_text(card)
        for sec in CARD_SECTIONS:
            if sec not in text:
                errors.append(f"{card}：缺少段落 {sec}")
        m = re.search(r"核对日期[:：]\s*(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})", text)
        if not m:
            warnings.append(f"{card}：没有写核对日期")
        else:
            age = (time.time() - time.mktime(time.strptime(m.group(1), "%Y-%m-%d"))) / 86400
            if age > 120:
                warnings.append(f"{card}：已经 {int(age)} 天没核对，代码可能变了，请用 kit-extract-pattern 重新核对")
    return count


def lint_registry() -> int:
    data = kitlib.load_json(kitlib.REGISTRY_PATH)
    if data is None:
        errors.append("registry.json 不存在")
        return 0
    templates = {p.stem for p in (ROOT / "templates").glob("*.md")}
    agents = {p.name[: -len(".agent.md")] for p in (ROOT / "agents").glob("*.agent.md")}
    ids: set[str] = set()
    actions = data.get("actions", [])
    for a in actions:
        aid = a.get("id", "")
        if not aid or aid in ids:
            errors.append(f"registry.json：按钮 id 为空或重复：{aid!r}")
        ids.add(aid)
        if a.get("template") and a["template"] not in templates:
            errors.append(f"registry.json：{aid} 引用的模板 {a['template']} 不存在")
        if a.get("agent") and a["agent"] not in agents:
            errors.append(f"registry.json：{aid} 引用的代理 {a['agent']} 不存在")
        if a.get("kind") == "run_tool":
            script = a.get("command", [""])[1] if len(a.get("command", [])) > 1 else ""
            script_path = Path(kitlib.expand(script, {"kit": str(ROOT)}))
            if script and script.endswith(".py") and not script_path.is_file():
                errors.append(f"registry.json：{aid} 的脚本不存在：{script}")
        if a.get("kind") not in {"launch_agent", "prompt_only", "run_tool", "open_path", "builtin"}:
            errors.append(f"registry.json：{aid} 的 kind 不认识：{a.get('kind')}")
    return len(actions)


def lint_templates() -> int:
    count = 0
    for t in sorted((ROOT / "templates").glob("*.md")):
        count += 1
        for ph in set(re.findall(r"\{\{(\w[\w-]*)\}\}", kitlib.read_text(t))):
            if ph not in KNOWN_PLACEHOLDERS:
                errors.append(f"{t}：未知占位符 {{{{{ph}}}}}")
    return count


def lint_json_and_data() -> int:
    count = 0
    for path in list(ROOT.glob("*.json")) + list(ROOT.glob("evals/cases/*.json")) + list(ROOT.glob(".github/plugin/*.json")):
        count += 1
        try:
            json.loads(kitlib.read_text(path))
        except ValueError as exc:
            errors.append(f"{path}：JSON 格式错误：{exc}")
    import mem

    for m in mem.load_all(include_archive=True):
        count += 1
        if not m.meta.get("subject") or m.meta.get("type") not in mem.TYPES:
            errors.append(f"{m.path}：记忆缺少 subject 或 type 不对")
    return count


def lint_encoding() -> int:
    files = check_chars.iter_files([str(ROOT)], set(check_chars.DEFAULT_EXTS))
    for f in files:
        for issue in check_chars.check_file(f):
            if issue.severity == "error":
                errors.append(f"{issue.path}:{issue.line} {issue.message}")
    return len(files)


def main() -> int:
    kitlib.setup_console()
    counts = {
        "技能": lint_skills(),
        "代理": lint_agents(),
        "风格卡片": lint_cards(),
        "按钮": lint_registry(),
        "模板": lint_templates(),
        "数据文件": lint_json_and_data(),
        "编码检查文件": lint_encoding(),
    }
    print("检查范围：" + "，".join(f"{k} {v}" for k, v in counts.items()))
    for e in errors:
        print(f"[错误] {e}")
    for w in warnings:
        print(f"[提醒] {w}")
    print(f"结果：错误 {len(errors)} 个，提醒 {len(warnings)} 个。")
    return 1 if errors else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
''',
    "tools/kitlib.py": r'''"""ai-dev-kit 公共函数库。

所有脚本（钩子、工具、挂件）都从这里读取配置、定位目录、运行命令。
只使用 Python 标准库，保证在离线、不能装包的电脑上也能运行。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import fnmatch
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

KIT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DATA_DIR = Path(os.environ.get("AI_DEV_KIT_HOME") or (Path.home() / ".ai-dev-kit"))
CONFIG_PATH = KIT_ROOT / "kit.config.json"
EXAMPLE_CONFIG_PATH = KIT_ROOT / "kit.config.example.json"
REGISTRY_PATH = KIT_ROOT / "registry.json"
SCOPE_DIRNAME = ".ai-dev-kit"
SCOPE_FILENAME = "scope.json"

IS_WINDOWS = os.name == "nt"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 路径与配置


def copilot_home() -> Path:
    """Copilot CLI 的配置目录。设置了 COPILOT_HOME 时以它为准。"""
    env = os.environ.get("COPILOT_HOME")
    return Path(env) if env else Path.home() / ".copilot"


def load_json(path: Path | str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
    """读取 JSON。文件不存在返回 default；带 BOM 的 UTF-8 也能读。"""
    try:
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return default


def save_json(path: Path | str, data: Any) -> None:
    """原子写入 JSON：先写临时文件再替换，UTF-8 无 BOM，换行统一为 LF。"""
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    with open(tmp, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
        json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        f.write("\n")
    os.replace(tmp, path)


def write_text(path: Path | str, text: str) -> None:
    """写文本文件：UTF-8 无 BOM，LF 换行，自动建目录。"""
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
        f.write(text)


def read_text(path: Path | str) -> str:
    """读文本：优先 UTF-8（容忍 BOM），失败再试 GBK，最后用替换字符兜底。"""
    raw = Path(path).read_bytes()
    return decode_bytes(raw)


def decode_bytes(raw: bytes) -> str:
    if raw.startswith(b"\xff\xfe") or raw.startswith(b"\xfe\xff"):
        return raw.decode("utf-16", errors="replace")
    for enc in ("utf-8-sig", "gbk"):
        try:
            return raw.decode(enc)
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            continue
    return raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")


def load_config() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """读取 kit.config.json；还没创建时退回示例配置，并做标记。"""
    cfg = load_json(CONFIG_PATH)
    if cfg is None:
        cfg = load_json(EXAMPLE_CONFIG_PATH, {}) or {}
        cfg["_from_example"] = True
    return cfg


def python_exe(cfg: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> str:
    """配置里写的 Python；没写就用当前解释器。"""
    cfg = cfg if cfg is not None else load_config()
    configured = (cfg.get("python") or "").strip()
    return configured or sys.executable


def norm(path: Path | str) -> str:
    """统一大小写和分隔符，用于比较 Windows 路径。"""
    return os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(str(path)))


def find_project(cfg: dict[str, Any], cwd: Path | str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """按当前目录匹配 kit.config.json 里登记的项目（取最长匹配）。"""
    current = norm(cwd)
    best: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    best_len = -1
    for proj in cfg.get("projects", []):
        root = norm(proj.get("path", ""))
        if not proj.get("path"):
            continue
        if current == root or current.startswith(root.rstrip("\\/") + os.sep):
            if len(root) > best_len:
                best, best_len = proj, len(root)
    return best


def get_project(cfg: dict[str, Any], name: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    for proj in cfg.get("projects", []):
        if proj.get("name") == name:
            return proj
    return None


def get_profile(cfg: dict[str, Any], name: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    profiles = cfg.get("profiles", {})
    key = name or cfg.get("default_profile") or next(iter(profiles), "")
    prof = dict(profiles.get(key, {}))
    prof["_name"] = key
    return prof


def expand(value: str, ctx: dict[str, str]) -> str:
    """替换 {python} {kit} {project} 这类占位符。"""
    out = value
    for key, val in ctx.items():
        out = out.replace("{" + key + "}", val)
    return out


def expand_cmd(cmd: list[str], ctx: dict[str, str]) -> list[str]:
    return [expand(part, ctx) for part in cmd]


def base_ctx(cfg: dict[str, Any], project: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> dict[str, str]:
    ctx = {"python": python_exe(cfg), "kit": str(KIT_ROOT)}
    if project:
        ctx["project"] = str(project.get("path", ""))
        ctx["git_root"] = str(project.get("git_root") or project.get("path", ""))
    return ctx


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 运行命令


def resolve_exe(name: str) -> str:
    """在 PATH 里找可执行文件（含 Windows 的 .cmd/.bat/.exe）。找不到原样返回。"""
    if os.path.sep in name or (IS_WINDOWS and "/" in name):
        return name
    found = shutil.which(name)
    return found or name


def run(
    cmd: list[str],
    cwd: Path | str | None = None,
    timeout: int = 900,
    env_extra: dict[str, str] | None = None,
) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """运行命令并返回 (退出码, 合并后的输出)。不经过 shell，避免引号和注入问题。"""
    if not cmd:
        return 2, "命令为空"
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env["PYTHONIOENCODING"] = "utf-8"
    env["PYTHONUTF8"] = "1"
    if env_extra:
        env.update(env_extra)
    real = [resolve_exe(cmd[0])] + list(cmd[1:])
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(
            real,
            cwd=str(cwd) if cwd else None,
            capture_output=True,
            timeout=timeout,
            env=env,
            shell=False,
        )
    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
        return 127, f"找不到命令：{cmd[0]}（{exc}）"
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return 124, f"命令超时（{timeout} 秒）：{' '.join(cmd)}"
    except OSError as exc:
        return 126, f"命令无法启动：{cmd[0]}（{exc}）"
    text = decode_bytes(proc.stdout or b"")
    err = decode_bytes(proc.stderr or b"")
    if err.strip():
        text = text + ("\n" if text and not text.endswith("\n") else "") + err
    return proc.returncode, text


def git(args: list[str], cwd: Path | str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """只读 git 调用的统一入口（本工具包从不执行 git 写操作）。"""
    return run(["git", "--no-pager"] + args, cwd=cwd, timeout=120)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 指纹（哈希缓存）


def tree_fingerprint(root: Path | str, extra: str = "") -> str:
    """工作区指纹：HEAD + 未提交改动的文件内容哈希。

    内容没变，指纹就不变，验收结果可以直接复用，不必重跑测试、
    也不必把几千行测试输出再塞进模型上下文。
    """
    root = Path(root)
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    h.update(extra.encode("utf-8"))
    code, head = git(["rev-parse", "HEAD"], root)
    h.update(head.strip().encode("utf-8") if code == 0 else b"no-git")
    code, status = git(["status", "--porcelain", "--untracked-files=all"], root)
    if code != 0:
        status = ""
    for line in sorted(status.splitlines()):
        h.update(line.encode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
        rel = line[3:].strip().strip('"')
        if " -> " in rel:
            rel = rel.split(" -> ", 1)[1]
        target = root / rel
        if target.is_file():
            try:
                if target.stat().st_size <= 5_000_000:
                    h.update(hashlib.sha256(target.read_bytes()).digest())
            except OSError:
                pass
    return h.hexdigest()[:24]


def cache_get(namespace: str, key: str) -> Any:
    data = load_json(DATA_DIR / "cache" / f"{namespace}.json", {}) or {}
    return data.get(key)


def cache_put(namespace: str, key: str, value: Any, keep: int = 50) -> None:
    path = DATA_DIR / "cache" / f"{namespace}.json"
    data = load_json(path, {}) or {}
    data[key] = value
    if len(data) > keep:
        for old in sorted(data, key=lambda k: str(data[k].get("ts", "")))[: len(data) - keep]:
            data.pop(old, None)
    save_json(path, data)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 修改范围（scope）


def find_scope(start: Path | str) -> tuple[Path | None, dict[str, Any] | None]:
    """从 start 往上找 .ai-dev-kit/scope.json，返回 (仓库根, 范围数据)。"""
    cur = Path(start).resolve()
    for folder in [cur] + list(cur.parents):
        candidate = folder / SCOPE_DIRNAME / SCOPE_FILENAME
        if candidate.is_file():
            return folder, load_json(candidate, {})
    return None, None


def rel_posix(path: Path | str, root: Path | str) -> str:
    """把绝对路径变成相对仓库根的 POSIX 路径；不在根下面就返回绝对路径。"""
    p = os.path.realpath(str(path))
    r = os.path.realpath(str(root))
    try:
        rel = os.path.relpath(p, r)
    except ValueError:
        return p.replace("\\", "/")
    if rel.startswith(".."):
        return p.replace("\\", "/")
    return rel.replace("\\", "/")


def match_any(rel: str, patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    """大小写不敏感的通配匹配。* 可以跨目录，所以 src/** 和 src/* 都能匹配子目录。"""
    target = rel.replace("\\", "/").lower()
    name = target.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
    for pat in patterns:
        pat_l = pat.replace("\\", "/").lower().strip()
        if not pat_l:
            continue
        if fnmatch.fnmatchcase(target, pat_l) or fnmatch.fnmatchcase(name, pat_l):
            return True
        if pat_l.endswith("/**") and target.startswith(pat_l[:-3].rstrip("/") + "/"):
            return True
    return False


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 脱敏与日志

_SECRET_PATTERNS = [
    (re.compile(r"(?i)(password|passwd|pwd|secret|token|api[_-]?key|apikey)(\s*[=:]\s*)([^\s'\";,]+)"), r"\1\2***"),
    (re.compile(r"(?i)([a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*://[^:/\s]+:)([^@\s]+)(@)"), r"\1***\3"),
    (re.compile(r"\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}\b"), "sk-***"),
    (re.compile(r"\b[A-Za-z0-9+/]{40,}={0,2}\b"), "***"),
    (re.compile(r"\b[0-9a-fA-F]{32,}\b"), "***"),
]


def redact(text: str, limit: int = 400) -> str:
    """去掉密码、密钥、连接串里的口令，再截断，用于日志和展示。"""
    out = text
    for pattern, repl in _SECRET_PATTERNS:
        out = pattern.sub(repl, out)
    if len(out) > limit:
        out = out[:limit] + f"…（已截断，共 {len(text)} 字）"
    return out


def log_event(kind: str, record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """追加一行 JSON 日志到 ~/.ai-dev-kit/logs/<kind>-日期.jsonl。写日志失败不影响主流程。"""
    try:
        day = time.strftime("%Y%m%d")
        path = DATA_DIR / "logs" / f"{kind}-{day}.jsonl"
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        record = dict(record)
        record.setdefault("ts", time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"))
        with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    except Exception:  # 日志不能影响钩子和工具的主逻辑
        pass


def now() -> str:
    return time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")


def today() -> str:
    return time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")


def setup_console() -> None:
    """让 Windows 终端正确显示中文。"""
    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
        if callable(reconfigure):
            try:
                reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
            except Exception:
                pass
''',
    "tools/kit_new.py": r'''"""脚手架：一条命令新增技能、代理、风格卡片、评估用例、挂件按钮或项目，格式自动正确。

这是“动态增量”的入口：以后想加新能力，不用改挂件代码，也不用记格式。

用法：
    python tools/kit_new.py skill  kit-api-doc  "根据接口代码生成接口文档。用于新增或修改接口之后。" [--button 写接口文档]
    python tools/kit_new.py agent  kit-sql-reviewer "只读审查 SQL 和迁移脚本。" --tools read,grep,glob
    python tools/kit_new.py card   style-tap-backend 09-redis-cache "Redis 缓存"
    python tools/kit_new.py pack   style-my-frontend "某前端项目的代码风格包"
    python tools/kit_new.py case   guard-xxx --type guard
    python tools/kit_new.py button my-tool "我的工具" --tool "{kit}/tools/my_tool.py" [--group tools]
    python tools/kit_new.py project my-service "C:/code/my-service" [--style-pack style-tap-backend]
每次新增后会自动运行 kit_lint 检查格式；技能和代理需要重新运行 install.py 才会装进 Copilot。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
import subprocess
import sys

import kitlib

ROOT = kitlib.KIT_ROOT
NAME_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$")


def _check_name(name: str) -> None:
    if not NAME_RE.match(name):
        raise SystemExit(f"名字只能用小写字母、数字和连字符：{name}")


def _registry() -> dict:
    return kitlib.load_json(kitlib.REGISTRY_PATH, {"version": 1, "groups": [], "actions": []})


def _add_action(action: dict) -> None:
    data = _registry()
    if any(a.get("id") == action["id"] for a in data.get("actions", [])):
        print(f"registry.json 里已经有按钮 {action['id']}，未重复添加。")
        return
    data.setdefault("actions", []).append(action)
    kitlib.save_json(kitlib.REGISTRY_PATH, data)
    print(f"已在 registry.json 新增按钮：{action['label']}")


def new_skill(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    _check_name(args.name)
    d = ROOT / "skills" / args.name
    if d.exists():
        raise SystemExit(f"已存在：{d}")
    kitlib.write_text(d / "SKILL.md", f"""---
name: {args.name}
description: {args.description}
---

# {args.title or args.name}

## 什么时候用
- （写清触发条件：用户说了什么、遇到什么情况时加载这个技能）

## 步骤
1. （第一步，写成可执行的动作）
2. （第二步）
3. （验证：怎样确认做对了）

## 输出格式
- （要交付什么，最后附核对表）

## 不要做
- （常见错误做法）
""")
    print(f"已创建技能：{d / 'SKILL.md'}")
    if args.button:
        _add_action({"id": args.name, "label": args.button, "group": args.group or "workflow", "icon": "✦",
                     "kind": "prompt_only", "prompt": f"加载技能 /{args.name}，完成下面的任务：\n{{task}}",
                     "description": args.description})


def new_agent(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    _check_name(args.name)
    path = ROOT / "agents" / f"{args.name}.agent.md"
    if path.exists():
        raise SystemExit(f"已存在：{path}")
    tools = ", ".join(f'"{t.strip()}"' for t in args.tools.split(",") if t.strip())
    kitlib.write_text(path, f"""---
name: {args.name}
description: {args.description}
tools: [{tools}]
strict-tools-list: true
include-custom-instructions: true
---

你是{args.title or args.name}。

## 职责
- （这个角色负责什么）

## 边界
- 只使用上面列出的工具组；需要别的权限时停下来说明。
- 不读取 .env 等可能含密钥的文件；不执行 git 写操作。

## 工作方式
1. （先做什么）
2. （再做什么）

## 输出
- 结论 + 证据（文件:行号 或命令原始输出）+ 核对表。
""")
    print(f"已创建代理：{path}")
    if args.button:
        _add_action({"id": args.name, "label": args.button, "group": args.group or "workflow", "icon": "◆",
                     "kind": "launch_agent", "agent": args.name, "template": args.template or "implement",
                     "description": args.description})


def new_card(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    pack = ROOT / "style-packs" / args.pack
    if not (pack / "SKILL.md").is_file():
        raise SystemExit(f"风格包不存在：{pack}（先用 pack 子命令创建）")
    path = pack / "patterns" / f"{args.card}.md"
    if path.exists():
        raise SystemExit(f"已存在：{path}")
    template = kitlib.read_text(ROOT / "style-packs" / "_template" / "patterns" / "card-template.md")
    kitlib.write_text(path, template.replace("卡片标题", args.title).replace("YYYY-MM-DD", kitlib.today()))
    print(f"已创建卡片：{path}\n记得在 {pack / 'SKILL.md'} 的卡片目录里加上这一行。")


def new_pack(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    _check_name(args.name)
    if not args.name.startswith("style-"):
        raise SystemExit("风格包名字请以 style- 开头，例如 style-my-service")
    pack = ROOT / "style-packs" / args.name
    if pack.exists():
        raise SystemExit(f"已存在：{pack}")
    template = kitlib.read_text(ROOT / "style-packs" / "_template" / "SKILL.md")
    kitlib.write_text(pack / "SKILL.md", template.replace("style-template", args.name).replace("某项目", args.description))
    (pack / "patterns").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    print(f"已创建风格包：{pack}\n下一步：用技能 /kit-extract-pattern 从项目代码里提炼卡片，或运行 kit_new.py card。")


def new_case(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    path = ROOT / "evals" / "cases" / f"{args.name}.json"
    if path.exists():
        raise SystemExit(f"已存在：{path}")
    skeletons = {
        "guard": {"payload": {"toolName": "powershell", "toolArgs": {"command": "（命令）"}}, "expect": "deny"},
        "prompt": {"text": "（含糊的需求草稿）", "expect_missing": ["done", "verify"]},
        "static": {"check": "chars", "before": {}, "after": {"src/a.py": "（改动后的内容）"}, "expect": "fail"},
        "output": {"check_name": "tests", "exit_code": 0, "output": "（测试命令的原始输出）", "expect": "fail"},
        "agent": {"agent": "kit-reviewer", "prompt": "（给评审员的输入）", "expect_all": ["（必须出现的关键词正则）"], "expect_none": []},
    }
    case = {"id": args.name, "type": args.type, "title": "（一句话说明这条用例防的是什么）",
            "incident": "（来自哪次事故）", **skeletons[args.type]}
    kitlib.save_json(path, case)
    print(f"已创建用例：{path}，填好内容后运行 python tools/run_evals.py")


def new_button(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    _check_name(args.name)
    action: dict = {"id": args.name, "label": args.label, "group": args.group or "tools", "icon": "▸",
                    "description": args.description or args.label}
    if args.tool:
        action.update({"kind": "run_tool", "command": ["{python}", args.tool] + (args.args or [])})
    elif args.agent:
        action.update({"kind": "launch_agent", "agent": args.agent, "template": args.template or "implement"})
    else:
        action.update({"kind": "prompt_only", "template": args.template or "implement"})
    _add_action(action)


def new_project(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    cfg = kitlib.load_json(kitlib.CONFIG_PATH)
    if cfg is None:
        raise SystemExit("还没有 kit.config.json，先运行 install.py")
    if kitlib.get_project(cfg, args.name):
        raise SystemExit(f"项目 {args.name} 已存在")
    cfg.setdefault("projects", []).append({
        "name": args.name, "path": args.path, "style_pack": args.style_pack or "",
        "python_cmd": ["uv", "run", "python"],
        "checks": {"tests": ["uv", "run", "pytest", "-q"], "lint": ["uv", "run", "ruff", "check", "."],
                   "chars": ["{python}", "{kit}/tools/check_chars.py", "src", "tests"]},
        "source_dirs": ["src"], "test_dirs": ["tests"], "protected": [], "notes": [],
        "isolated_import_modules": [],
    })
    kitlib.save_json(kitlib.CONFIG_PATH, cfg)
    print(f"已登记项目 {args.name}。请打开 kit.config.json 按实际情况修改测试命令和受保护目录。")


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    kitlib.setup_console()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="ai-dev-kit 脚手架")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    p = sub.add_parser("skill"); p.add_argument("name"); p.add_argument("description")
    p.add_argument("--title"); p.add_argument("--button"); p.add_argument("--group")
    p = sub.add_parser("agent"); p.add_argument("name"); p.add_argument("description")
    p.add_argument("--tools", default="read,grep,glob"); p.add_argument("--title"); p.add_argument("--button")
    p.add_argument("--group"); p.add_argument("--template")
    p = sub.add_parser("card"); p.add_argument("pack"); p.add_argument("card"); p.add_argument("title")
    p = sub.add_parser("pack"); p.add_argument("name"); p.add_argument("description")
    p = sub.add_parser("case"); p.add_argument("name"); p.add_argument("--type", choices=["guard", "prompt", "static", "output", "agent"], default="guard")
    p = sub.add_parser("button"); p.add_argument("name"); p.add_argument("label"); p.add_argument("--tool")
    p.add_argument("--args", nargs="*"); p.add_argument("--agent"); p.add_argument("--template"); p.add_argument("--group")
    p.add_argument("--description")
    p = sub.add_parser("project"); p.add_argument("name"); p.add_argument("path"); p.add_argument("--style-pack")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    {"skill": new_skill, "agent": new_agent, "card": new_card, "pack": new_pack, "case": new_case,
     "button": new_button, "project": new_project}[args.cmd](args)
    if args.cmd != "project":
        print("\n运行格式检查：")
        subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / "tools" / "kit_lint.py")])
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
''',
    "tools/prompt_lint.py": r'''"""提示词体检（离线、零 token）：检查一段需求描述缺了什么，给出具体追问，再补全成 R3 提示词。

为什么先做本地体检：GitHub 官方的省额度建议里写到，提示词含糊时，代理要自己猜意图、
多读文件、反复返工，最费 token；而写清"目标、相关上下文、完成标准"几乎不增加 token，
却能大幅减少返工次数。所以在发给模型之前先把需求补完整，是成本最低的一步。

检查 8 个维度：目标、位置、背景、约束、完成标准、验证方式、输出要求、示例。
另外识别含糊词（"优化一下""看看""等等"）和"一句话里塞了好几件事"。

用法：
    python prompt_lint.py "帮我优化一下上传接口"
    python prompt_lint.py --file draft.txt --r3 --project tap-ai-backend
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

import kitlib


@dataclass
class Dimension:
    key: str
    label: str
    weight: int
    pattern: re.Pattern[str]
    question: str
    example: str


DIMENSIONS = [
    Dimension("goal", "目标", 20,
              re.compile(r"(实现|新增|增加|添加|修复|修改|改成|改为|删除|移除|重构|迁移|编写|写一个|写个|生成|排查|定位|评审|审查|检查|整理|设计|支持|接入|拆分|合并|替换|升级|补充|implement|add|fix|remove|refactor|migrate|write|review)", re.I),
              "这次具体要做成什么？用一句话说出动作和对象。",
              "例：给 /tap/ai/library/kb/documents 新增 delete 接口"),
    Dimension("location", "位置", 15,
              re.compile(r"([\w\-]+/[\w\-./]+|\b[\w\-]+\.(py|ts|js|md|json|toml|sql|yaml|yml)\b|模块|目录|文件|接口|函数|方法|类|路由|表|字段|页面|组件)", re.I),
              "涉及哪些文件、模块或接口？不知道具体文件时，说出模块或接口路径也行。",
              "例：src/tap_ai/api/library_kb.py、application/library_kb.py"),
    Dimension("context", "背景", 10,
              re.compile(r"(因为|背景|现状|目前|现在|之前|原来|报错|错误|异常|问题是|原因|发现|出现|导致|traceback|error)", re.I),
              "现在是什么情况？有报错就贴报错原文，有现象就描述现象。",
              "例：上传 100 个文件有 70 个失败，报错是 MySQL server has gone away"),
    Dimension("constraints", "约束", 12,
              re.compile(r"(不要|禁止|不能|不许|只能|只改|只允许|必须|保持|不改|别动|不得|仅|兼容|不影响)", re.I),
              "有哪些不能做的事？比如不能改哪些文件、不能加依赖、不能改接口格式。",
              "例：不改问答插件；不新增依赖；接口返回格式保持不变"),
    Dimension("done", "完成标准", 15,
              re.compile(r"(验收|完成标准|做到|预期|期望|应该|成功后|结果是|达到|满足|通过|返回.{0,10}(为|是)|算完成)", re.I),
              "怎样才算完成？写出能检查的结果。",
              "例：重复上传同名文件时返回 skipped，document_id 不变"),
    Dimension("verify", "验证方式", 12,
              re.compile(r"(pytest|单元测试|测试|ruff|mypy|运行|执行|命令|curl|调用一次|截图|日志|run_checks)", re.I),
              "用什么方法验证？跑哪些测试或命令？",
              "例：uv run pytest tests/unit/test_library_kb_service.py -q 全部通过"),
    Dimension("output", "输出要求", 8,
              re.compile(r"(报告|格式|核对表|列出|贴出|表格|清单|markdown|返回给我|告诉我|说明)", re.I),
              "做完要给你什么？比如改动摘要、原始输出、核对表。",
              "例：贴出 git diff --stat 和测试最后 15 行，最后给核对表"),
    Dimension("examples", "示例", 8,
              re.compile(r"(例如|比如|样例|示例|如下|参考|照着|类似|```|输入[:：]|输出[:：])", re.I),
              "有没有可以参考的例子、样例数据或已有写法？",
              "例：写法参考 library_kb.py 里的 documents/retry 接口"),
]

VAGUE = ["优化一下", "优化下", "处理一下", "处理下", "看看", "看一下", "完善一下", "完善下", "搞一下", "弄一下",
         "搞定", "等等", "之类", "什么的", "好一点", "更好", "尽量", "差不多", "相关的", "一些", "适当", "合理",
         "大概", "整一下", "调整一下", "改一改", "弄好"]
SPLITTERS = re.compile(r"(然后|还有|另外|以及|同时|再就是|并且|；|;|\n\s*\d+[.、)])")


def analyze(text: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    text = text.strip()
    dims: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    score = 0
    for d in DIMENSIONS:
        hit = bool(d.pattern.search(text))
        if d.key == "goal" and len(text) < 12:
            hit = False
        if hit:
            score += d.weight
        dims.append({"key": d.key, "label": d.label, "ok": hit, "question": d.question, "example": d.example})
    vague = [w for w in VAGUE if w in text]
    score -= min(20, 4 * len(vague))
    tasks = len(SPLITTERS.findall(text)) + 1
    score = max(0, min(100, score))
    level = "好" if score >= 75 else ("一般" if score >= 50 else "需要补充")
    advice: list[str] = []
    for d in dims:
        if not d["ok"]:
            advice.append(f"缺少【{d['label']}】：{d['question']}（{d['example']}）")
    if vague:
        advice.append(f"含糊的说法：{'、'.join(vague)}。请换成具体的动作和结果，例如把“优化一下”改成“把接口响应时间从 3 秒降到 1 秒以内”。")
    if tasks >= 3:
        advice.append(f"一段话里大约有 {tasks} 件事。模型容易只做前一两件，建议编号列出，或者拆成几个任务分开做。")
    return {"score": score, "level": level, "dimensions": dims, "vague": vague, "task_count": tasks, "advice": advice}


def build_r3(text: str, answers: dict[str, str] | None = None, project: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
             token_mode: str = "full") -> str:
    """把草稿和补充回答拼成 R3 提示词；仍然缺的信息写成【待补充】，提醒你填。"""
    answers = answers or {}
    report = analyze(text + "\n" + "\n".join(answers.values()))
    missing = {d["key"] for d in report["dimensions"] if not d["ok"]}

    def val(key: str, fallback: str) -> str:
        v = answers.get(key, "").strip()
        if v:
            return v
        return f"【待补充：{fallback}】" if key in missing else "（见任务描述）"

    items = [x.strip() for x in re.split(r"\n\s*\d+[.、)]\s*|；|;", text) if x.strip()]
    numbered = "\n".join(f"{i}. {x}" for i, x in enumerate(items, start=1)) if len(items) > 1 else f"1. {text}"
    proj_line = ""
    if project:
        proj_line = f"\n【工作目录】{project.get('path')}"
        if project.get("style_pack"):
            proj_line += f"\n【风格包】开工前先加载技能 /{project['style_pack']}，按里面的卡片写代码。"
    protected = ""
    if project and project.get("protected"):
        protected = "\n- 不改这些受保护区域：" + "、".join(project["protected"])
    output_rule = ("只输出：改动摘要、命令输出的关键行（失败行和汇总行）、核对表。不要复述代码。"
                   if token_mode == "lean" else "按通用规则贴原始输出，最后给核对表。")
    checklist_rows = "\n".join(f"| {i} | | |" for i in range(1, len(items) + 1 if len(items) > 1 else 2))
    return f"""【任务】{items[0][:60] if items else text[:60]}{proj_line}

【背景】
{val('context', '现在是什么情况、有什么报错')}

【绝对禁止】
- 不读 .env 等可能含密钥的文件；不连接外部服务；不安装任何包
- git 只允许 status / diff / log / show / grep / ls-files
- 不改 pyproject.toml、uv.lock；不对已有文件运行 ruff format{protected}
- {val('constraints', '还有哪些不能做的事')}

【本次允许修改的文件】
{val('location', '涉及哪些文件；名单以外的文件一律不改，需要改就停下来告诉我')}

【要做的 {max(1, len(items))} 件事】
{numbered}

【完成标准】
{val('done', '怎样才算完成')}

【验证】
{val('verify', '跑哪些测试或命令，要求贴原始输出')}

【参考】
{val('examples', '可参考的已有写法或样例')}

【完成报告】
{val('output', output_rule)}

【核对表】
| 编号 | 状态（完成 / 未找到 / 未做 / 已停下） | 证据（文件:行号 或 命令） |
{checklist_rows}
"""


def format_report(rep: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    lines = [f"提示词得分：{rep['score']} / 100（{rep['level']}）"]
    for d in rep["dimensions"]:
        lines.append(f"  {'✓' if d['ok'] else '✗'} {d['label']}")
    if rep["advice"]:
        lines.append("建议补充：")
        lines.extend(f"  - {a}" for a in rep["advice"])
    return "\n".join(lines)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    kitlib.setup_console()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="提示词体检（离线）")
    parser.add_argument("text", nargs="?", help="需求草稿")
    parser.add_argument("--file", help="从文件读取草稿")
    parser.add_argument("--r3", action="store_true", help="同时输出补全后的 R3 提示词")
    parser.add_argument("--project")
    parser.add_argument("--lean", action="store_true", help="省 token 版输出要求")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    text = kitlib.read_text(args.file) if args.file else (args.text or sys.stdin.read())
    rep = analyze(text)
    print(format_report(rep))
    if args.r3:
        proj = kitlib.get_project(kitlib.load_config(), args.project) if args.project else None
        print("\n" + build_r3(text, project=proj, token_mode="lean" if args.lean else "full"))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
''',
    "tools/selftest.py": r'''"""工具包自身的自测：在临时目录里把每个模块跑一遍，确认在这台电脑上工作正常。

不会碰你的项目、Copilot 配置和记忆库（日志、缓存也写到临时目录）。
用法：python tools/selftest.py
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sys
import tempfile
import traceback
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable

_TMP_HOME = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="kit-selftest-home-")
os.environ["AI_DEV_KIT_HOME"] = _TMP_HOME  # 必须在导入 kitlib 之前设置
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "hooks"))

import check_chars
import checks
import kitlib
import mem
import prompt_builder
import prompt_lint

passed: list[str] = []
failed: list[tuple[str, str]] = []


def case(name: str) -> Callable[[Callable[[], None]], Callable[[], None]]:
    def deco(fn: Callable[[], None]) -> Callable[[], None]:
        try:
            fn()
            passed.append(name)
        except Exception:
            failed.append((name, traceback.format_exc(limit=3)))
        return fn
    return deco


def expect(cond: bool, msg: str) -> None:
    if not cond:
        raise AssertionError(msg)


@case("check_chars：识别 UTF-16、BOM、零宽字符、Tab 缩进，正常文件不误报")
def _t_chars() -> None:
    d = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
    (d / "u16.py").write_bytes("x = 1\n".encode("utf-16"))
    (d / "bom.py").write_bytes(b"\xef\xbb\xbfx = 1\n")
    (d / "zw.py").write_text("x\u200b = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (d / "tab.py").write_text("def f():\n\treturn 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (d / "ok.py").write_text("def f():\n    return '中文'\n", encoding="utf-8")
    rules = {p.name: {i.rule for i in check_chars.check_file(p)} for p in d.iterdir()}
    expect("utf16" in rules["u16.py"], f"UTF-16 未识别：{rules['u16.py']}")
    expect("bom" in rules["bom.py"], "BOM 未识别")
    expect("zero-width" in rules["zw.py"], "零宽字符未识别")
    expect("tab-indent" in rules["tab.py"], "Tab 缩进未识别")
    expect(not rules["ok.py"], f"正常文件被误报：{rules['ok.py']}")


@case("守卫：内置 27 条拦截/放行用例全部符合预期")
def _t_guard() -> None:
    import guard

    g = guard.Guard({"guard": {}, "projects": []})
    sandbox = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    bad = []
    for title, payload, expected in guard.SELFTEST_CASES:
        p = dict(payload)
        p["cwd"] = sandbox
        decision, _, _ = g.decide(p)
        if decision != expected:
            bad.append(f"{title}: 期望 {expected} 实际 {decision}")
    expect(not bad, "；".join(bad))


@case("守卫：修改范围（scope）外的文件被拒绝，范围内放行")
def _t_scope_guard() -> None:
    import guard

    root = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
    kitlib.save_json(root / ".ai-dev-kit" / "scope.json", {"files": ["src/a.py"], "globs": ["tests/*"]})
    g = guard.Guard({"guard": {}, "projects": []})
    inside = {"toolName": "edit", "toolArgs": {"path": str(root / "src" / "a.py"), "new_str": "x = 1"}, "cwd": str(root)}
    glob_ok = {"toolName": "edit", "toolArgs": {"path": str(root / "tests" / "test_a.py"), "new_str": "x = 1"}, "cwd": str(root)}
    outside = {"toolName": "edit", "toolArgs": {"path": str(root / "src" / "b.py"), "new_str": "x = 1"}, "cwd": str(root)}
    expect(g.decide(inside)[0] == "none", "范围内文件被误拒")
    expect(g.decide(glob_ok)[0] == "none", "通配范围内文件被误拒")
    expect(g.decide(outside)[0] == "deny", "范围外文件没有被拒绝")


@case("守卫：受保护区域（同事的模块）被拒绝")
def _t_protected_area() -> None:
    import guard

    root = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
    cfg = {"guard": {}, "projects": [{"name": "p", "path": str(root), "protected": ["src/qa/**"]}]}
    g = guard.Guard(cfg)
    payload = {"toolName": "edit", "toolArgs": {"path": str(root / "src" / "qa" / "x.py"), "new_str": "y = 2"}, "cwd": str(root)}
    expect(g.decide(payload)[0] == "deny", "受保护区域没有被拒绝")


@case("记忆：新增、检索、引用核对（有效 / 锚点移动 / 失效）、拒绝密钥")
def _t_mem() -> None:
    tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
    mem.MEMORY_DIR = tmp / "memory"
    mem.ARCHIVE_DIR = mem.MEMORY_DIR / "_archive"
    proj = tmp / "proj"
    kitlib.write_text(proj / "src" / "a.py", "line1\nline2\nVERSION = 'v2'\n")
    orig_root = mem.project_root
    mem.project_root = lambda name: proj if name != mem.GLOBAL else kitlib.KIT_ROOT
    try:
        rc = mem.main(["add", "--project", "p", "--type", "lesson", "--subject", "版本号要同步",
                       "--fact", "VERSION 修改时文档也要改", "--cite", "src/a.py:3#VERSION", "--tags", "版本"])
        expect(rc == 0, f"新增失败 rc={rc}")
        found = mem.search("版本号", project="p")
        expect(found and "版本号" in str(found[0][1].meta.get("subject")), "检索不到刚新增的记忆")
        m = found[0][1]
        expect(mem.verify_memory(m)[0] == "ok", "引用应当有效")
        kitlib.write_text(proj / "src" / "a.py", "\n" * 60 + "VERSION = 'v3'\n")
        status, _ = mem.check_citation("src/a.py:3#VERSION", proj)
        expect(status == "moved", f"锚点移动应识别为 moved，实际 {status}")
        kitlib.write_text(proj / "src" / "a.py", "nothing\n")
        expect(mem.verify_memory(m)[0] == "stale", "引用失效应识别为 stale")
        expect("版本号" not in mem.digest("p", str(proj)), "失效记忆不应出现在会话摘要里")
        rc = mem.main(["add", "--project", "p", "--type", "fact", "--subject", "数据库", "--fact",
                       "连接串 mysql://user:pass123@host/db", "--cite", "user:测试"])
        expect(rc == 2, "含口令的内容应被拒绝保存")
    finally:
        mem.project_root = orig_root


@case("提示词体检：含糊需求得分低并给出追问；具体需求得分高")
def _t_prompt_lint() -> None:
    vague = prompt_lint.analyze("帮我优化一下上传接口")
    good = prompt_lint.analyze(
        "修复 src/tap_ai/api/library_kb.py 的上传接口：目前同名文件重复上传会报错。不要改问答插件。"
        "完成标准：重复上传返回 skipped，document_id 不变。验证：运行 uv run pytest tests/unit/test_library_kb_service.py。"
        "最后贴出测试输出和核对表。例如参考 documents/retry 的写法。")
    expect(vague["score"] < 50, f"含糊需求得分过高：{vague['score']}")
    expect(any("含糊" in a for a in vague["advice"]), "没有指出含糊词")
    expect(good["score"] >= 75, f"具体需求得分过低：{good['score']}")
    r3 = prompt_lint.build_r3("帮我优化一下上传接口")
    expect("【待补充" in r3 and "【核对表】" in r3, "R3 补全结果缺少待补充标记或核对表")


@case("提示词构建：每个模板都能生成，且通用规则在最前面、没有残留占位符")
def _t_builder() -> None:
    for action in prompt_builder.list_actions():
        text = prompt_builder.build(action, "测试任务")
        expect("{{" not in text, f"{action} 有残留占位符")
        if "{{rules}}" in kitlib.read_text(prompt_builder.TEMPLATE_DIR / f"{action}.md"):
            expect(text.startswith("【通用规则】"), f"{action} 的通用规则不在开头（影响缓存命中）")


@case("静态检查：嵌套 ORM 类、永远成立的断言、getattr(settings)")
def _t_checks() -> None:
    root = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
    kitlib.write_text(root / "src" / "models.py",
                      "class Base: pass\nclass TokenUsage(Base):\n    __tablename__ = 't'\n"
                      "    class LibraryChunkDraft(Base):\n        __tablename__ = 'd'\n")
    kitlib.write_text(root / "tests" / "test_x.py", "def test_a():\n    x = 1\n    assert x == x\n")
    project = {"name": "t", "path": str(root), "source_dirs": ["src"], "test_dirs": ["tests"]}
    ctx = checks.Ctx(project, root, root, "HEAD", {"projects": [project]})
    res = {r.name: r for r in checks.run_checks(ctx, ["nested_orm_class", "tautological_asserts"])}
    expect(not res["nested_orm_class"].ok, "没识别出嵌套的 ORM 类")
    expect(not res["tautological_asserts"].ok, "没识别出永远成立的断言")
    expect(checks.GETATTR_SETTINGS.search('x = getattr(settings, "library_kb_enabled", False)') is not None,
           "getattr(settings) 规则失效")


@case("指纹缓存：内容不变指纹不变，内容变了指纹改变")
def _t_fingerprint() -> None:
    root = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
    code, _ = kitlib.git(["init", "-q"], root)
    if code != 0:
        return  # 没有 git 时跳过
    kitlib.write_text(root / "a.txt", "1")
    f1 = kitlib.tree_fingerprint(root)
    f2 = kitlib.tree_fingerprint(root)
    kitlib.write_text(root / "a.txt", "2")
    f3 = kitlib.tree_fingerprint(root)
    expect(f1 == f2, "内容没变指纹却变了")
    expect(f1 != f3, "内容变了指纹却没变")


@case("工具包文件：编码全部正常")
def _t_kit_encoding() -> None:
    files = check_chars.iter_files([str(kitlib.KIT_ROOT)], set(check_chars.DEFAULT_EXTS))
    bad = [f"{i.path}:{i.line} {i.message}" for f in files for i in check_chars.check_file(f) if i.severity == "error"]
    expect(not bad, "；".join(bad[:5]))


def main() -> int:
    kitlib.setup_console()
    for name in passed:
        print(f"[通过] {name}")
    for name, tb in failed:
        print(f"[失败] {name}\n{tb}")
    print(f"\n自测结果：{len(passed)} 通过，{len(failed)} 失败。（临时目录：{_TMP_HOME}）")
    if failed:
        print("请把上面的失败信息整段发给维护者。")
    return 1 if failed else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
''',
    "tools/run_evals.py": r'''"""评估运行器：用真实事故做成的用例，确认工具包的防线一直有效（改了技能、规则、检查之后必跑）。

用例类型（evals/cases/*.json）：
    guard   给守卫一个模拟的工具调用，期望 deny 或 none
    prompt  给提示词体检一段草稿，期望识别出缺少的维度
    static  在临时 git 仓库里重现事故（before → after），期望静态检查报出问题（fail）或不报（pass）
    output  给一段测试命令的输出，期望一键验证判定为失败（例如 0 条测试、收集中断）或通过
    agent   真正调用 Copilot 的某个自定义代理（非交互模式），用正则检查回答里该有/不该有的内容
            这类会消耗额度，默认不跑，加 --agent 才跑

依据：Anthropic 的评估实践建议从 20～50 个真实失败案例起步；能用代码判分就用代码判分；
回归用例的通过率应接近 100%；分数之外还要读执行记录。agent 类的完整输出会保存下来供阅读。

用法：
    python tools/run_evals.py                 # 跑 guard / prompt / static
    python tools/run_evals.py --only guard
    python tools/run_evals.py --agent --profile latest   # 额外跑 agent 类
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import re
import sys
import tempfile
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "hooks"))

import checks
import kitlib
import prompt_lint
from guard import Guard

CASE_DIR = kitlib.KIT_ROOT / "evals" / "cases"


def _fill_cwd(value: Any, cwd: str) -> Any:
    """把用例里的 {cwd} 换成临时目录，方便写“项目内的绝对路径”。"""
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return value.replace("{cwd}", cwd.replace("\\", "/"))
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_fill_cwd(v, cwd) for v in value]
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {k: _fill_cwd(v, cwd) for k, v in value.items()}
    return value


def run_guard(case: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    cwd = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="kit-eval-")
    projects = []
    if case.get("protected"):
        projects.append({"name": "eval", "path": cwd, "protected": case["protected"],
                         "protected_allow": case.get("protected_allow", [])})
    if case.get("scope"):
        kitlib.save_json(Path(cwd) / kitlib.SCOPE_DIRNAME / kitlib.SCOPE_FILENAME, case["scope"])
    guard = Guard({"guard": case.get("guard_config", {}), "projects": projects})
    payload = _fill_cwd(dict(case["payload"]), cwd)
    payload["cwd"] = cwd
    decision, reason, rule = guard.decide(payload)
    return decision == case["expect"], f"期望 {case['expect']}，实际 {decision} {rule}"


def run_output(case: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    """把一段测试命令的输出交给 run_checks 的结果判断，确认假通过能被识别。"""
    import run_checks

    res = run_checks._summarize(case.get("check_name", "tests"), int(case.get("exit_code", 0)), case["output"])
    flagged = not res["ok"]
    ok = flagged if case["expect"] == "fail" else not flagged
    return ok, f"判定 {'失败' if flagged else '通过'}；识别出：{res['problems'] or '无'}"


def _write_fixture(path: Path, content: Any) -> None:
    """内容可以是字符串（UTF-8 写入），也可以是 {"text": ..., "encoding": "utf-16"} 来制造编码事故。"""
    if isinstance(content, dict):
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_bytes(str(content["text"]).encode(content.get("encoding", "utf-8")))
    else:
        kitlib.write_text(path, content)


def run_prompt(case: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    rep = prompt_lint.analyze(case["text"])
    missing = {d["key"] for d in rep["dimensions"] if not d["ok"]}
    expected = set(case.get("expect_missing", []))
    not_flagged = expected - missing
    ok = not not_flagged
    if "expect_max_score" in case:
        ok = ok and rep["score"] <= case["expect_max_score"]
    if "expect_min_score" in case:
        ok = ok and rep["score"] >= case["expect_min_score"]
    return ok, f"得分 {rep['score']}，识别为缺少：{sorted(missing)}" + (f"；没识别出：{sorted(not_flagged)}" if not_flagged else "")


def _git(args: list[str], cwd: Path) -> None:
    code, out = kitlib.git(["-c", "user.name=kit", "-c", "user.email=kit@local"] + args, cwd)
    if code != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"git {' '.join(args)} 失败：{out[:200]}")


def run_static(case: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="kit-eval-repo-"))
    _git(["init", "-q"], tmp)
    for rel, content in case.get("before", {}).items():
        _write_fixture(tmp / rel, content)
    kitlib.write_text(tmp / ".gitkeep", "")
    _git(["add", "-A"], tmp)
    _git(["commit", "-q", "-m", "before"], tmp)
    for rel, content in case.get("after", {}).items():
        if content is None:
            (tmp / rel).unlink(missing_ok=True)
        else:
            _write_fixture(tmp / rel, content)
    if case.get("scope"):
        kitlib.save_json(tmp / kitlib.SCOPE_DIRNAME / kitlib.SCOPE_FILENAME, case["scope"])
    project = {"name": "eval", "path": str(tmp), "source_dirs": ["src"], "test_dirs": ["tests"]}
    project.update(case.get("project", {}))
    ctx = checks.Ctx(project, tmp, tmp, "HEAD", {"projects": [project]})
    result = checks.run_checks(ctx, [case["check"]])[0]
    flagged = (not result.ok) and not result.skipped
    ok = flagged if case["expect"] == "fail" else not flagged
    return ok, f"{result.summary}" + (f"；{result.findings[0]}" if result.findings else "")


def run_agent(case: dict[str, Any], profile_name: str | None) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    cfg = kitlib.load_config()
    profile = kitlib.get_profile(cfg, profile_name)
    template = profile.get("noninteractive_cmd")
    if not template:
        return False, f"配置 {profile['_name']} 没有 noninteractive_cmd，无法自动运行"
    ctx = kitlib.base_ctx(cfg)
    ctx.update({"agent": case["agent"], "prompt": case["prompt"], "launcher": profile.get("launcher", "copilot")})
    cmd = kitlib.expand_cmd(list(template), ctx)
    started = time.time()
    code, out = kitlib.run(cmd, cwd=tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="kit-eval-agent-"), timeout=int(case.get("timeout", 600)))
    log = kitlib.DATA_DIR / "evals" / f"{case['id']}-{time.strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')}.txt"
    kitlib.write_text(log, f"命令：{cmd}\n退出码：{code}\n耗时：{time.time() - started:.0f}s\n\n{out}")
