    missing = [p for p in case.get("expect_all", []) if not re.search(p, out, re.I | re.S)]
    present = [p for p in case.get("expect_none", []) if re.search(p, out, re.I | re.S)]
    ok = code == 0 and not missing and not present
    detail = f"输出已保存：{log}"
    if missing:
        detail += f"；缺少：{missing}"
    if present:
        detail += f"；不该出现：{present}"
    return ok, detail


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    kitlib.setup_console()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="运行评估用例")
    parser.add_argument("--only", help="只跑某类：guard,prompt,static,output,agent")
    parser.add_argument("--agent", action="store_true", help="同时运行 agent 类（会消耗额度）")
    parser.add_argument("--profile", help="agent 类使用的配置，默认 default_profile")
    parser.add_argument("--case", help="只跑某个用例 id")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    only = set(args.only.split(",")) if args.only else None
    cases = []
    for path in sorted(CASE_DIR.glob("*.json")):
        case = kitlib.load_json(path)
        case.setdefault("id", path.stem)
        cases.append(case)
    stats: dict[str, list[int]] = {}
    for case in cases:
        ctype = case.get("type", "")
        if args.case and case["id"] != args.case:
            continue
        if only and ctype not in only:
            continue
        if ctype == "agent" and not args.agent:
            continue
        try:
            if ctype == "guard":
                ok, detail = run_guard(case)
            elif ctype == "prompt":
                ok, detail = run_prompt(case)
            elif ctype == "static":
                ok, detail = run_static(case)
            elif ctype == "output":
                ok, detail = run_output(case)
            elif ctype == "agent":
                ok, detail = run_agent(case, args.profile)
            else:
                ok, detail = False, f"未知类型 {ctype}"
        except Exception as exc:
            ok, detail = False, f"用例运行出错：{exc!r}"
        s = stats.setdefault(ctype, [0, 0])
        s[0] += int(ok)
        s[1] += 1
        print(f"[{'通过' if ok else '失败'}] {case['id']}（{ctype}）{case.get('title', '')}\n       {detail}")
    total_ok = sum(v[0] for v in stats.values())
    total = sum(v[1] for v in stats.values())
    print("\n" + "，".join(f"{k} {v[0]}/{v[1]}" for k, v in sorted(stats.items())))
    print(f"总计 {total_ok}/{total} 通过。" + ("" if args.agent else "（agent 类未运行，加 --agent 运行）"))
    return 0 if total_ok == total else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
''',
    "tools/kit_doctor.py": r'''"""体检：检查工具包在这台电脑上是否装好、能用，每一项都给出修复办法。只读，不改任何东西。

用法：python tools/kit_doctor.py [--quick]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable

import kitlib

OK, WARN, FAIL = "通过", "提醒", "失败"
results: list[tuple[str, str, str, str]] = []  # (状态, 项目, 说明, 修复办法)


def add(status: str, item: str, detail: str, fix: str = "") -> None:
    results.append((status, item, detail, fix))


def check_python() -> None:
    v = sys.version_info
    if v >= (3, 9):
        add(OK, "Python", f"{v.major}.{v.minor}.{v.micro}　{sys.executable}")
    else:
        add(FAIL, "Python", f"版本 {v.major}.{v.minor} 太旧", "需要 Python 3.9 以上")
    cfg = kitlib.load_config()
    configured = cfg.get("python", "")
    if configured and not Path(configured).exists():
        add(FAIL, "配置里的 Python", f"找不到 {configured}", "修改 kit.config.json 的 python 字段")


def check_tkinter() -> None:
    try:
        import tkinter

        add(OK, "tkinter（挂件需要）", f"Tk {tkinter.TkVersion}")
    except Exception as exc:
        add(WARN, "tkinter（挂件需要）", f"无法导入：{exc}", "挂件用不了，其他功能不受影响；重新安装 Python 时勾选 tcl/tk")


def check_git() -> None:
    code, out = kitlib.run(["git", "--version"], timeout=20)
    add(OK if code == 0 else FAIL, "git", out.strip() if code == 0 else "找不到 git", "" if code == 0 else "安装 git 或把它加进 PATH")


def check_copilot(cfg: dict) -> None:
    for name, prof in (cfg.get("profiles") or {}).items():
        launcher = prof.get("launcher", "")
        label = f"Copilot（{prof.get('label', name)}）"
        if not launcher:
            add(WARN, label, "没有配置 launcher", "在 kit.config.json 的 profiles 里填写启动命令或 bat 路径")
            continue
        if prof.get("launcher_type") == "bat":
            exists = Path(launcher).is_file()
            add(OK if exists else FAIL, label, f"启动脚本 {'存在' if exists else '不存在'}：{launcher}（不自动运行，避免弹出会话）",
                "" if exists else "修改 profiles 里的 launcher 路径")
            continue
        code, out = kitlib.run([launcher, "--version"], timeout=30)
        if code == 0:
            add(OK, label, out.strip().splitlines()[0] if out.strip() else "可运行")
        else:
            add(FAIL, label, f"运行 {launcher} --version 失败：{out.strip()[:120]}", "确认 copilot 在 PATH 里，或填写完整路径")


def check_install() -> None:
    home = kitlib.copilot_home()
    manifest = kitlib.load_json(kitlib.DATA_DIR / "installed.json")
    if not manifest:
        add(FAIL, "安装状态", "还没有安装", "运行 python tools/install.py")
        return
    if Path(manifest.get("kit_root", "")).resolve() != kitlib.KIT_ROOT.resolve():
        add(WARN, "安装状态", f"安装时的工具包目录是 {manifest.get('kit_root')}，与当前目录不同", "在当前目录重新运行 install.py")
    missing_skills = [s for s in manifest.get("skills", []) if not (home / "skills" / s / "SKILL.md").is_file()]
    add(OK if not missing_skills else FAIL, "技能", f"已安装 {len(manifest.get('skills', []))} 个"
        + (f"，缺少：{', '.join(missing_skills)}" if missing_skills else ""), "" if not missing_skills else "重新运行 install.py")
    missing_agents = [a for a in manifest.get("agents", []) if not (home / "agents" / a).is_file()]
    add(OK if not missing_agents else FAIL, "自定义代理", f"已安装 {len(manifest.get('agents', []))} 个"
        + (f"，缺少：{', '.join(missing_agents)}" if missing_agents else ""), "" if not missing_agents else "重新运行 install.py")
    instr = home / "copilot-instructions.md"
    has_block = instr.is_file() and "ai-dev-kit:begin" in kitlib.read_text(instr)
    add(OK if has_block else FAIL, "个人指令", str(instr) if has_block else "没有找到工具包指令段", "" if has_block else "重新运行 install.py")
    hook_file = home / "hooks" / "ai-dev-kit.json"
    if manifest.get("hooks"):
        try:
            data = json.loads(kitlib.read_text(hook_file))
            events = sorted(data.get("hooks", {}).keys())
            scripts = [a for evs in data["hooks"].values() for e in evs for a in e.get("args", [])]
            bad = [s for s in scripts if not Path(s).is_file()]
            add(OK if not bad else FAIL, "钩子配置", f"{hook_file}：{', '.join(events)}" + (f"；脚本不存在：{bad}" if bad else ""),
                "" if not bad else "重新运行 install.py")
        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
            add(FAIL, "钩子配置", f"读取失败：{exc}", "重新运行 install.py")
    else:
        add(WARN, "钩子配置", "安装时选择了不注册钩子：硬拦截没有生效", "需要时运行 python tools/install.py（不加 --no-hooks）")


def check_guard() -> None:
    script = kitlib.KIT_ROOT / "hooks" / "guard.py"
    code, out = kitlib.run([sys.executable, str(script), "--selftest"], timeout=60)
    last = out.strip().splitlines()[-1] if out.strip() else ""
    add(OK if code == 0 else FAIL, "守卫自测", last or out[:200], "" if code == 0 else "把输出发给维护者；必要时先用 --no-hooks 重新安装")
    payload = json.dumps({"toolName": "view", "toolArgs": {"path": ".env"}, "cwd": str(Path.home())})
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(script)], input=payload.encode("utf-8"),
                              capture_output=True, timeout=10)
        reply = proc.stdout.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        ok = proc.returncode == 0 and '"deny"' in reply
        add(OK if ok else FAIL, "钩子通信（模拟 Copilot 调用）", reply[:120] or proc.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace")[:120],
            "" if ok else "守卫没有按预期输出 JSON，检查 Python 路径和脚本")
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        add(FAIL, "钩子通信（模拟 Copilot 调用）", "超时", "钩子超时会被 Copilot 当成放行，需要排查")


def check_projects(cfg: dict) -> None:
    home = kitlib.copilot_home()
    for proj in cfg.get("projects", []):
        name = proj.get("name", "?")
        path = Path(proj.get("path", ""))
        if not path.is_dir():
            add(FAIL, f"项目 {name}", f"目录不存在：{path}", "修改 kit.config.json")
            continue
        notes = []
        pack = proj.get("style_pack")
        if pack and not (home / "skills" / pack / "SKILL.md").is_file():
            notes.append(f"风格包 {pack} 未安装")
        for key, cmd in (proj.get("checks") or {}).items():
            exe = kitlib.expand(cmd[0], kitlib.base_ctx(cfg, proj)) if cmd else ""
            if exe and kitlib.resolve_exe(exe) == exe and not Path(exe).exists():
                notes.append(f"{key} 命令找不到：{exe}")
        add(OK if not notes else WARN, f"项目 {name}", str(path) + ("；" + "；".join(notes) if notes else ""),
            "" if not notes else "检查 kit.config.json 中该项目的 style_pack 和 checks")


def check_kit_files() -> None:
    import check_chars

    files = check_chars.iter_files([str(kitlib.KIT_ROOT)], set(check_chars.DEFAULT_EXTS))
    errors = [i for f in files for i in check_chars.check_file(f) if i.severity == "error"]
    add(OK if not errors else FAIL, "工具包文件编码", f"{len(files)} 个文件，问题 {len(errors)} 处",
        "" if not errors else "运行 python tools/check_chars.py . 查看")


def check_data_dir() -> None:
    try:
        probe = kitlib.DATA_DIR / "logs" / ".probe"
        kitlib.write_text(probe, "ok")
        probe.unlink()
        add(OK, "数据目录", str(kitlib.DATA_DIR))
    except OSError as exc:
        add(FAIL, "数据目录", f"无法写入 {kitlib.DATA_DIR}：{exc}", "设置环境变量 AI_DEV_KIT_HOME 指向可写目录")


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    kitlib.setup_console()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="ai-dev-kit 体检")
    parser.add_argument("--quick", action="store_true", help="跳过运行 copilot --version")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    cfg = kitlib.load_config()
    if cfg.get("_from_example"):
        add(WARN, "配置文件", "还没有 kit.config.json，正在使用示例配置", "运行 install.py 会自动创建，然后按实际情况修改")
    steps: list[Callable[[], None]] = [check_python, check_tkinter, check_git, check_data_dir, check_install,
                                       check_guard, check_kit_files, lambda: check_projects(cfg)]
    if not args.quick:
        steps.insert(3, lambda: check_copilot(cfg))
    for step in steps:
        try:
            step()
        except Exception as exc:
            add(FAIL, getattr(step, "__name__", "检查"), f"检查本身出错：{exc!r}")
    width = max(len(r[1]) for r in results) + 2
    for status, item, detail, fix in results:
        print(f"[{status}] {item.ljust(width)}{detail}")
        if fix and status != OK:
            print(f"       {''.ljust(width)}→ {fix}")
    fails = sum(1 for r in results if r[0] == FAIL)
    warns = sum(1 for r in results if r[0] == WARN)
    print(f"\n体检完成：失败 {fails} 项，提醒 {warns} 项。")
    print("手动确认两件事（脚本没法替你看）：\n"
          "  1. 在 Copilot 会话里输入 /skills list，能看到 kit- 开头的技能；输入 /agent，能看到 kit- 开头的代理。\n"
          "  2. 在会话里让 Copilot “读取 .env 文件”，应该看到 ai-dev-kit 守卫的拒绝提示。")
    return 1 if fails else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
''',
    "tools/mem.py": r'''"""记忆系统：把"踩过的坑、做过的决定、团队约定"变成可验证、可检索、可淘汰的记录。

设计依据（详见 docs/05-记忆系统.md）：
- 存成人能读、能改、能进版本库的 Markdown 文件，一条一个文件，多人协作不冲突；
- 每条记忆带"引用"（文件:行号#锚点文字），使用前当场核对引用是否还成立，
  这是 GitHub Copilot Memory 公开的做法（存的时候带出处，用的时候即时验证）；
- 会话开始只注入少量高优先级、已验证的记忆，其余按需检索，控制 token；
- 有"取代""过期""归档"机制，旧知识不会一直堆在那里误导模型。

用法：
    python mem.py add --project tap-ai-backend --type lesson --subject "..." --fact "..." \
        --why "..." --how "..." --cite "src/a.py:12#关键字" --tags "导入,循环"
    python mem.py search "循环导入" [--project P]
    python mem.py list [--project P] [--status active]
    python mem.py verify [--project P] [--write]
    python mem.py show ID
    python mem.py supersede OLD_ID --by NEW_ID
    python mem.py archive ID
    python mem.py prune [--days 30] [--write]
    python mem.py stats
    python mem.py digest --project P        # 预览会话开始时会注入的内容
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import math
import re
import shutil
import sys
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import kitlib

MEMORY_DIR = kitlib.KIT_ROOT / "memory"
ARCHIVE_DIR = MEMORY_DIR / "_archive"
GLOBAL = "_global"
TYPES = {
    "lesson": "教训",
    "decision": "决定",
    "convention": "约定",
    "fact": "事实",
    "preference": "偏好",
}
PRIORITY_WEIGHT = {"high": 3.0, "normal": 1.5, "low": 0.5}
SUBJECT_MAX = 60
FACT_MAX = 400


@dataclass
class Memory:
    path: Path
    meta: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    body: str = ""

    @property
    def id(self) -> str:
        return str(self.meta.get("id") or self.path.stem)

    @property
    def project(self) -> str:
        return str(self.meta.get("project") or GLOBAL)

    @property
    def status(self) -> str:
        return str(self.meta.get("status") or "active")

    @property
    def citations(self) -> list[str]:
        cites = self.meta.get("citations") or []
        return cites if isinstance(cites, list) else [str(cites)]

    @property
    def fact(self) -> str:
        m = re.search(r"(?m)^事实[:：]\s*(.+)$", self.body)
        if m:
            return m.group(1).strip()
        first = next((ln.strip() for ln in self.body.splitlines() if ln.strip()), "")
        return first


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 读写


def parse(path: Path) -> Memory:
    text = kitlib.read_text(path)
    meta: dict[str, Any] = {}
    body = text
    if text.startswith("---"):
        parts = text.split("\n---", 1)
        header = parts[0][3:]
        body = parts[1].lstrip("-").lstrip("\n") if len(parts) > 1 else ""
        current_list: str | None = None
        for line in header.splitlines():
            if not line.strip():
                continue
            if line.lstrip().startswith("- ") and current_list:
                meta.setdefault(current_list, []).append(line.lstrip()[2:].strip())
                continue
            if ":" in line:
                key, _, value = line.partition(":")
                key = key.strip()
                value = value.strip()
                if value == "":
                    current_list = key
                    meta[key] = []
                else:
                    current_list = None
                    meta[key] = value
    return Memory(path, meta, body.strip())


def render(mem: Memory) -> str:
    order = ["id", "type", "project", "subject", "tags", "status", "priority", "created",
             "verified", "expires", "supersedes", "superseded_by", "source"]
    lines = ["---"]
    for key in order:
        if key in mem.meta and mem.meta[key] not in (None, ""):
            lines.append(f"{key}: {mem.meta[key]}")
    for key, value in mem.meta.items():
        if key not in order and key != "citations" and not isinstance(value, list):
            lines.append(f"{key}: {value}")
    lines.append("citations:")
    for cite in mem.citations:
        lines.append(f"  - {cite}")
    lines.append("---")
    lines.append(mem.body.strip())
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"


def save(mem: Memory) -> None:
    kitlib.write_text(mem.path, render(mem))


def load_all(project: str | None = None, include_archive: bool = False) -> list[Memory]:
    items: list[Memory] = []
    if not MEMORY_DIR.is_dir():
        return items
    for path in sorted(MEMORY_DIR.rglob("*.md")):
        if path.name.lower() == "readme.md":
            continue
        if not include_archive and ARCHIVE_DIR in path.parents:
            continue
        try:
            m = parse(path)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if project and m.project not in {project, GLOBAL}:
            continue
        items.append(m)
    return items


def find(mem_id: str) -> Memory | None:
    for m in load_all(include_archive=True):
        if m.id == mem_id:
            return m
    return None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 引用核对

CITE_RE = re.compile(r"^(?P<path>.+?)(?::(?P<line>\d+))?(?:#(?P<anchor>.+))?$")


def project_root(project: str) -> Path:
    if project == GLOBAL:
        return kitlib.KIT_ROOT
    proj = kitlib.get_project(kitlib.load_config(), project)
    if proj and proj.get("path"):
        return Path(proj["path"])
    return kitlib.KIT_ROOT


def check_citation(cite: str, root: Path) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """返回 (状态, 说明)。状态：ok / moved / stale / missing / user（来自用户口述，无法核对）。"""
    cite = cite.strip()
    if cite.startswith(("user:", "用户:", "用户：")):
        return "user", "来自用户说明"
    m = CITE_RE.match(cite)
    if not m:
        return "stale", "引用格式不对"
    path = root / m.group("path").strip()
    if not path.is_file():
        return "missing", f"文件不存在：{m.group('path')}"
    anchor = (m.group("anchor") or "").strip()
    if not anchor:
        return "ok", "文件存在"
    try:
        lines = kitlib.read_text(path).splitlines()
    except OSError as exc:
        return "stale", f"读不了文件：{exc}"
    line_no = int(m.group("line") or 0)
    if line_no:
        lo, hi = max(0, line_no - 21), min(len(lines), line_no + 20)
        if any(anchor in ln for ln in lines[lo:hi]):
            return "ok", "锚点仍在原位置附近"
    for idx, ln in enumerate(lines, start=1):
        if anchor in ln:
            return "moved", f"锚点移到了第 {idx} 行"
    return "stale", f"找不到锚点文字：{anchor}"


def verify_memory(mem: Memory) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
    """一条记忆的总体状态：任何一个引用失效，就是 stale。"""
    root = project_root(mem.project)
    notes: list[str] = []
    overall = "ok"
    for cite in mem.citations:
        status, note = check_citation(cite, root)
        notes.append(f"{cite} → {status}（{note}）")
        if status in {"stale", "missing"}:
            overall = "stale"
    if not mem.citations:
        overall = "uncited"
    return overall, notes


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 检索


def tokens(text: str) -> list[str]:
    """英文按词，中文按相邻两字切分（离线也能做像样的中文检索）。"""
    text = text.lower()
    words = re.findall(r"[a-z0-9_.\-]{2,}", text)
    cjk = re.findall("[\u4e00-\u9fff]+", text)
    grams: list[str] = []
    for run in cjk:
        if len(run) == 1:
            grams.append(run)
        grams.extend(run[i:i + 2] for i in range(len(run) - 1))
    return words + grams


def score(mem: Memory, query_tokens: list[str]) -> float:
    if not query_tokens:
        return 0.0
    fields = {
        "subject": (str(mem.meta.get("subject", "")), 3.0),
        "tags": (str(mem.meta.get("tags", "")), 2.0),
        "body": (mem.body, 1.0),
    }
    total = 0.0
    for text, weight in fields.values():
        bag = tokens(text)
        if not bag:
            continue
        for qt in set(query_tokens):
            hits = bag.count(qt)
            if hits:
                total += weight * (1 + math.log(hits))
    total *= PRIORITY_WEIGHT.get(str(mem.meta.get("priority", "normal")), 1.0) / 1.5
    return total


def search(query: str, project: str | None = None, limit: int = 8) -> list[tuple[float, Memory]]:
    qt = tokens(query)
    ranked = [(score(m, qt), m) for m in load_all(project) if m.status == "active"]
    ranked = [r for r in ranked if r[0] > 0]
    ranked.sort(key=lambda r: (-r[0], r[1].id))
    return ranked[:limit]


def _short_cite(cites: list[str]) -> str:
    if not cites:
        return ""
    first = cites[0].split("#", 1)[0]
    return f"（见 {first}{' 等' if len(cites) > 1 else ''}）"


def digest(project: str | None, project_path: str | None = None,
           max_chars: int = 1200, max_items: int = 8) -> str:
    """会话开始时注入的摘要：只要 active、引用核对通过的条目，按优先级和核对日期排序。"""
    candidates = [m for m in load_all(project) if m.status == "active"]
    if project is None:
        candidates = [m for m in candidates if m.project == GLOBAL]
    # 先按核对日期从新到旧，再按优先级从高到低（排序是稳定的，所以优先级相同时新的在前）
    candidates.sort(key=lambda m: str(m.meta.get("verified", "")), reverse=True)
    candidates.sort(key=lambda m: -PRIORITY_WEIGHT.get(str(m.meta.get("priority", "normal")), 1.0))
    lines: list[str] = []
    used = 0
    for m in candidates:
        if len(lines) >= max_items:
            break
        state, _ = verify_memory(m)
        if state == "stale":
            continue
        label = TYPES.get(str(m.meta.get("type", "")), "记忆")
        line = f"- [{label}] {m.meta.get('subject', m.id)}：{m.fact}{_short_cite(m.citations)}"
        if len(line) > 220:
            line = line[:217] + "…"
        if used + len(line) > max_chars:
            break
        lines.append(line)
        used += len(line) + 1
    return "\n".join(lines)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 命令

_SECRET_HINT = re.compile(r"(?i)(password|passwd|api[_-]?key|secret|token)\s*[=:]\s*\S+|[a-z]+://[^:\s]+:[^@\s]+@|\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}")


def _slug(text: str) -> str:
    ascii_part = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "-", text.lower()).strip("-")
    if ascii_part:
        return ascii_part[:40]
    return "m" + hashlib.sha1(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:8]


def cmd_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    project = args.project or GLOBAL
    if args.type not in TYPES:
        print(f"类型只能是：{', '.join(TYPES)}")
        return 2
    subject = args.subject.strip()
    fact = args.fact.strip()
    if not subject or not fact:
        print("subject 和 fact 都不能为空。")
        return 2
    if len(subject) > SUBJECT_MAX:
        print(f"subject 太长（{len(subject)} 字），请控制在 {SUBJECT_MAX} 字内：一句话说清是什么。")
        return 2
    if len(fact) > FACT_MAX:
        print(f"fact 太长（{len(fact)} 字），请控制在 {FACT_MAX} 字内；细节放进 --why / --how。")
        return 2
    joined = " ".join([subject, fact, args.why or "", args.how or ""] + list(args.cite or []))
    if _SECRET_HINT.search(joined):
        print("拒绝保存：内容里像是有密码、密钥或带口令的连接串。记忆里绝对不能存密钥。")
        return 2
    if not args.cite and args.type in {"lesson", "convention", "fact"}:
        print("提醒：这条记忆没有引用。没有引用就无法自动核对，建议加 --cite \"路径:行号#锚点文字\"；"
              "如果来自用户口述，用 --cite \"user:日期 用户说明\"。")
    similar = search(subject + " " + fact, project=project if project != GLOBAL else None, limit=3)
    if similar and similar[0][0] >= 6:
        print("发现内容相近的已有记忆，请确认不是重复（如果是更新，用 supersede 取代旧条目）：")
        for s, m in similar:
            print(f"  {m.id}（相似度 {s:.1f}）：{m.meta.get('subject')}")
        if not args.force:
            print("加 --force 仍然保存。")
            return 3
    mem_id = f"{kitlib.today()}-{_slug(args.id or subject)}"
    target = MEMORY_DIR / project / f"{mem_id}.md"
    n = 2
    while target.exists():
        target = MEMORY_DIR / project / f"{mem_id}-{n}.md"
        n += 1
    body_lines = [f"事实：{fact}"]
    if args.why:
        body_lines.append(f"原因：{args.why.strip()}")
    if args.how:
        body_lines.append(f"怎么用：{args.how.strip()}")
    mem = Memory(target, {
        "id": target.stem, "type": args.type, "project": project, "subject": subject,
        "tags": args.tags or "", "status": "active", "priority": args.priority,
        "created": kitlib.today(), "verified": kitlib.today(), "source": args.source or "",
        "citations": list(args.cite or []),
    }, "\n".join(body_lines))
    state, notes = verify_memory(mem)
    if state == "stale":
        print("引用核对失败，未保存：")
        for note in notes:
            print("  " + note)
        return 4
    save(mem)
    print(f"已保存：{target}")
    for note in notes:
        print("  " + note)
    return 0


def cmd_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    items = load_all(args.project, include_archive=args.all)
    if args.status:
        items = [m for m in items if m.status == args.status]
    for m in items:
        print(f"{m.id} [{m.project}/{m.meta.get('type')}/{m.status}/{m.meta.get('priority', 'normal')}] {m.meta.get('subject')}")
    print(f"共 {len(items)} 条。")
    return 0


def cmd_search(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    results = search(args.query, args.project, args.limit)
    if not results:
        print("没有找到相关记忆。")
        return 0
    for s, m in results:
        print(f"{m.id}（{s:.1f}）[{TYPES.get(str(m.meta.get('type')), '')}] {m.meta.get('subject')}")
        print(f"    {m.fact}")
        if m.citations:
            print(f"    引用：{'；'.join(m.citations)}")
    return 0


def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    m = find(args.id)
    if not m:
        print("找不到这条记忆。")
        return 1
    print(render(m))
    state, notes = verify_memory(m)
    print(f"核对结果：{state}")
    for note in notes:
        print("  " + note)
    return 0


def cmd_verify(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    items = [m for m in load_all(args.project) if m.status in {"active", "stale"}]
    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    for m in items:
        state, notes = verify_memory(m)
        counts[state] = counts.get(state, 0) + 1
        if state != "ok":
            print(f"{m.id} → {state}")
            for note in notes:
                print("    " + note)
        if args.write:
            if state == "stale" and m.status == "active":
                m.meta["status"] = "stale"
                save(m)
            elif state in {"ok", "uncited"}:
                m.meta["verified"] = kitlib.today()
                if m.status == "stale":
                    m.meta["status"] = "active"
                save(m)
    summary = "，".join(f"{k} {v} 条" for k, v in sorted(counts.items()))
    print(f"核对 {len(items)} 条：{summary or '无'}。" + ("" if args.write else "（加 --write 才会更新状态）"))
    return 0


def cmd_supersede(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    old, new = find(args.old), find(args.by)
    if not old or not new:
        print("找不到对应的记忆。")
        return 1
    old.meta["status"] = "superseded"
    old.meta["superseded_by"] = new.id
    new.meta["supersedes"] = old.id
    save(old)
    save(new)
    print(f"{old.id} 已被 {new.id} 取代。")
    return 0


def _archive(m: Memory) -> Path:
    target = ARCHIVE_DIR / m.project / m.path.name
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    m.meta["archived"] = kitlib.today()
    save(m)
    shutil.move(str(m.path), str(target))
    return target


def cmd_archive(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    m = find(args.id)
    if not m:
        print("找不到这条记忆。")
        return 1
    print(f"已归档到：{_archive(m)}")
    return 0


def cmd_prune(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    cutoff = time.time() - args.days * 86400
    picked: list[Memory] = []
    for m in load_all():
        expires = str(m.meta.get("expires", ""))
        expired = bool(expires) and expires < kitlib.today()
        old_stale = m.status in {"stale", "superseded"} and m.path.stat().st_mtime < cutoff
        if expired or old_stale:
            picked.append(m)
    for m in picked:
        print(f"{'归档' if args.write else '将归档'}：{m.id}（{m.status}）")
        if args.write:
            _archive(m)
    print(f"共 {len(picked)} 条。" + ("" if args.write else "（加 --write 才会执行）"))
    return 0


def cmd_stats(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    items = load_all(include_archive=True)
    by: dict[tuple[str, str], int] = {}
    for m in items:
        by[(m.project, m.status)] = by.get((m.project, m.status), 0) + 1
    for (proj, status), n in sorted(by.items()):
        print(f"{proj:<24} {status:<11} {n}")
    print(f"总计 {len(items)} 条。")
    return 0


def cmd_digest(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    proj = kitlib.get_project(kitlib.load_config(), args.project) if args.project else None
    text = digest(args.project, proj.get("path") if proj else None, args.max_chars, args.max_items)
    print(text or "（没有可注入的记忆）")
    print(f"\n——共 {len(text)} 字")
    return 0


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    kitlib.setup_console()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="ai-dev-kit 记忆系统")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)

    p = sub.add_parser("add", help="新增一条记忆")
    p.add_argument("--project", help="项目名；不填表示全局")
    p.add_argument("--type", required=True, choices=sorted(TYPES))
    p.add_argument("--subject", required=True, help="一句话主题（≤60 字）")
    p.add_argument("--fact", required=True, help="事实本身（≤400 字）")
    p.add_argument("--why", help="为什么重要")
    p.add_argument("--how", help="以后遇到时怎么做")
    p.add_argument("--cite", action="append", help="引用：路径:行号#锚点文字，可多次")
    p.add_argument("--tags", default="")
    p.add_argument("--priority", default="normal", choices=["high", "normal", "low"])
    p.add_argument("--source", help="来源，如：修1b 事故、用户 11-02 确认")
    p.add_argument("--id", help="自定义英文短名")
    p.add_argument("--force", action="store_true")
    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_add)

    p = sub.add_parser("list")
    p.add_argument("--project")
    p.add_argument("--status")
    p.add_argument("--all", action="store_true", help="包含已归档")
    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_list)

    p = sub.add_parser("search")
    p.add_argument("query")
    p.add_argument("--project")
    p.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=8)
    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_search)

    p = sub.add_parser("show")
    p.add_argument("id")
    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_show)

    p = sub.add_parser("verify")
    p.add_argument("--project")
    p.add_argument("--write", action="store_true")
    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_verify)

    p = sub.add_parser("supersede")
    p.add_argument("old")
    p.add_argument("--by", required=True)
    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_supersede)

    p = sub.add_parser("archive")
    p.add_argument("id")
    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_archive)

    p = sub.add_parser("prune")
    p.add_argument("--days", type=int, default=30)
    p.add_argument("--write", action="store_true")
    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_prune)

    p = sub.add_parser("stats")
    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_stats)

    p = sub.add_parser("digest")
    p.add_argument("--project")
    p.add_argument("--max-chars", type=int, default=1200)
    p.add_argument("--max-items", type=int, default=8)
    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_digest)

    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    return int(args.func(args))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
''',
    "tools/run_checks.py": r'''"""一键验收：按 kit.config.json 里登记的命令跑测试、lint、类型检查和静态检查，输出精简报告。

为什么不让模型直接跑 pytest：
- 完整测试输出动辄几千行，全塞进上下文既费 token，又容易让模型抓不住重点；
- 这里只保留"失败行 + 汇总行 + 最后几行"，完整输出存到文件里，需要时再看；
- 工作区指纹（HEAD + 改动文件内容的哈希）没变时，直接复用上一次结果，不重复跑。

还会主动识别这几种"看起来通过、其实没跑"的情况：
    0 个测试被收集 / 收集阶段报错 / 全部被跳过 / 命令找不到

用法：
    python run_checks.py --project tap-ai-backend          # 默认：所有登记的命令 + 快速静态检查
    python run_checks.py --project tap-ai-backend --only tests,lint
    python run_checks.py --project tap-ai-backend --force  # 忽略缓存，强制重跑
    python run_checks.py --project tap-ai-backend --full   # 额外跑较慢的静态检查
退出码：0 全部通过；1 有失败。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import checks
import kitlib

FAIL_LINE = re.compile(r"^(FAILED|ERROR|E\s{3}|.*\berror\b:|.*: error:|Found \d+ error|\S+\.py:\d+:\d+: [A-Z]+\d+)", re.I)
SUMMARY_LINE = re.compile(r"(\d+ (passed|failed|error|errors|skipped|xfailed|xpassed|deselected|warnings?)|"
                          r"no tests ran|tests? collected|Success: no issues|All checks passed|Found \d+ errors?|"
                          r"扫描 \d+ 个文件)", re.I)


def _summarize(name: str, code: int, out: str, tail: int = 8) -> dict[str, Any]:
    lines = out.splitlines()
    fails = [ln for ln in lines if FAIL_LINE.search(ln)]
    summary = [ln for ln in lines if SUMMARY_LINE.search(ln)]
    problems: list[str] = []
    low = out.lower()
    if name == "tests":
        if "no tests ran" in low or re.search(r"\b0 (tests? )?(passed|collected)\b", low) or "collected 0 items" in low:
            problems.append("一个测试都没有运行：请检查工作目录和测试路径")
        if "interrupted" in low and "error" in low:
            problems.append("测试在收集阶段被中断：报错的测试文件一个都没跑")
        m_pass = re.search(r"(\d+) passed", low)
        m_skip = re.search(r"(\d+) skipped", low)
        if m_skip and not m_pass:
            problems.append("所有测试都被跳过，等于没跑")
    if code == 127:
        problems.append("命令找不到：检查 kit.config.json 里的命令和 PATH")
    ok = code == 0 and not problems
    return {
        "name": name, "ok": ok, "code": code, "problems": problems,
        "fail_lines": fails[:40], "summary_lines": summary[-5:], "tail": lines[-tail:],
    }


def run_project(project_name: str, only: list[str] | None, force: bool, full: bool) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    cfg = kitlib.load_config()
    project = kitlib.get_project(cfg, project_name)
    if not project:
        return False, f"kit.config.json 里没有登记项目：{project_name}"
    root = Path(project["path"])
    if not root.is_dir():
        return False, f"项目目录不存在：{root}"
    commands: dict[str, list[str]] = dict(project.get("checks", {}))
    commands.pop("collect", None)
    selected = [n for n in commands if not only or n in only]
    static_names = (list(checks.CHECKS) if full else list(checks.FAST_CHECKS))
    if only:
        static_names = [n for n in static_names if n in only or "static" in only]
    key_extra = f"{project_name}|{','.join(selected)}|{','.join(static_names)}|{kitlib.load_json(kitlib.CONFIG_PATH, {}).get('kit_version', '')}"
    fingerprint = kitlib.tree_fingerprint(root, key_extra)
    cached = None if force else kitlib.cache_get("verify", fingerprint)
    if cached and cached.get("ok"):
        return True, (f"[缓存命中] 工作区自 {cached['ts']} 验收通过后没有任何变化（指纹 {fingerprint}），直接复用结果。\n"
                      + cached["report"] + "\n（需要强制重跑请加 --force）")

    ctx = kitlib.base_ctx(cfg, project)
    report_parts: list[str] = [f"项目：{project_name}　目录：{root}　指纹：{fingerprint}"]
    full_log: list[str] = []
    all_ok = True
    for name in selected:
        cmd = kitlib.expand_cmd(list(commands[name]), ctx)
        started = time.time()
        code, out = kitlib.run(cmd, cwd=root, timeout=int(project.get("check_timeout", 1800)))
        elapsed = time.time() - started
        full_log.append(f"===== {name}: {' '.join(cmd)} （退出码 {code}，{elapsed:.1f}s）\n{out}")
        s = _summarize(name, code, out)
        all_ok = all_ok and s["ok"]
        mark = "通过" if s["ok"] else "失败"
        report_parts.append(f"[{mark}] {name}（退出码 {code}，{elapsed:.0f}s）：{' '.join(cmd)}")
        for p in s["problems"]:
            report_parts.append(f"    ! {p}")
        for ln in s["summary_lines"]:
            report_parts.append(f"    = {ln.strip()[:200]}")
        if not s["ok"]:
            for ln in s["fail_lines"][:25] or s["tail"]:
                report_parts.append(f"    > {ln.rstrip()[:220]}")

    if static_names:
        cctx = checks.Ctx(project, root, Path(project.get("git_root") or root), "HEAD", cfg)
        results = checks.run_checks(cctx, static_names)
        report_parts.append("静态检查：")
        report_parts.append(checks.format_results(results))
        all_ok = all_ok and not any((not r.ok) and (not r.skipped) and r.severity == "error" for r in results)

    report = "\n".join(report_parts)
    log_path = kitlib.DATA_DIR / "verify" / f"{project_name}-{time.strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')}.log"
    kitlib.write_text(log_path, "\n\n".join(full_log) + "\n\n" + report + "\n")
    report += f"\n完整输出：{log_path}"
    report += "\n结论：" + ("全部通过" if all_ok else "有失败，见上面带 > 的行")
    kitlib.cache_put("verify", fingerprint, {"ok": all_ok, "ts": kitlib.now(), "report": report})
    return all_ok, report


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    kitlib.setup_console()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="一键验收（测试 + lint + 类型 + 静态检查）")
    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--only", help="逗号分隔：tests,lint,types,chars,static 或某个静态检查名")
    parser.add_argument("--force", action="store_true", help="忽略缓存")
    parser.add_argument("--full", action="store_true", help="包含较慢的静态检查")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    only = [x.strip() for x in args.only.split(",")] if args.only else None
    ok, report = run_project(args.project, only, args.force, args.full)
    print(report)
    return 0 if ok else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
''',
    "tools/check_chars.py": r'''"""编码与隐形字符检查（只读）。

能发现：UTF-16 文件、带 BOM 的 UTF-8、无法按 UTF-8 解码的文件、
零宽字符、不间断空格、全角空格、Python 文件里的 Tab 缩进、
Python 里的非 ASCII 标识符、同一文件混用 CRLF 和 LF。

用法：
    python check_chars.py src tests sql
    python check_chars.py --json src
退出码：0 = 没有问题；1 = 发现问题；2 = 参数错误。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import io
import json
import os
import sys
import tokenize
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from pathlib import Path

DEFAULT_EXTS = {
    ".py", ".pyi", ".md", ".txt", ".json", ".toml", ".yaml", ".yml", ".sql",
    ".ini", ".cfg", ".ps1", ".bat", ".cmd", ".sh", ".html", ".css", ".js", ".ts",
    ".csv", ".xml", ".example",
}
SKIP_DIRS = {".git", ".venv", "venv", "__pycache__", "node_modules", ".mypy_cache",
             ".ruff_cache", ".pytest_cache", "dist", "build", ".idea", ".ai-dev-kit"}
# 这些文件里可能有密钥，检查工具也不打开
SECRET_NAMES = {".env"}

ZERO_WIDTH = {
    "\u200b": "零宽空格 U+200B",
    "\u200c": "零宽非连接符 U+200C",
    "\u200d": "零宽连接符 U+200D",
    "\u2060": "词连接符 U+2060",
    "\ufeff": "文件中间的 BOM U+FEFF",
    "\u00ad": "软连字符 U+00AD",
}
NBSP = "\u00a0"
FULLWIDTH_SPACE = "\u3000"


@dataclass
class Issue:
    path: str
    line: int
    rule: str
    message: str
    severity: str = "error"  # error / warning


def _is_secret_file(path: Path) -> bool:
    name = path.name.lower()
    return name in SECRET_NAMES or (name.startswith(".env.") and name != ".env.example")


def check_bytes(path: str, raw: bytes, is_python: bool) -> list[Issue]:
    issues: list[Issue] = []
    if raw.startswith(b"\xff\xfe") or raw.startswith(b"\xfe\xff"):
        issues.append(Issue(path, 1, "utf16", "文件是 UTF-16 编码，应转成 UTF-8（无 BOM）"))
        return issues
    if b"\x00" in raw[:4096]:
        issues.append(Issue(path, 1, "nul", "文件里有 NUL 字节，可能是 UTF-16 或二进制文件"))
        return issues
    body = raw
    if raw.startswith(b"\xef\xbb\xbf"):
        issues.append(Issue(path, 1, "bom", "文件开头有 UTF-8 BOM，应去掉", "warning"))
        body = raw[3:]
    try:
        text = body.decode("utf-8")
    except UnicodeDecodeError as exc:
        line = body[: exc.start].count(b"\n") + 1
        issues.append(Issue(path, line, "not-utf8", f"不是合法 UTF-8（第 {exc.start} 字节附近），可能是 GBK 编码"))
        return issues

    crlf = text.count("\r\n")
    lf = text.count("\n") - crlf
    if crlf and lf:
        issues.append(Issue(path, 1, "mixed-eol", f"混用换行符：CRLF {crlf} 处，LF {lf} 处", "warning"))

    for lineno, line in enumerate(text.splitlines(), start=1):
        for ch, label in ZERO_WIDTH.items():
            if ch in line:
                issues.append(Issue(path, lineno, "zero-width", f"含隐形字符：{label}"))
        if NBSP in line:
            sev = "error" if is_python else "warning"
            issues.append(Issue(path, lineno, "nbsp", "含不间断空格 U+00A0（看起来像空格，实际不是）", sev))
        if FULLWIDTH_SPACE in line:
            stripped = line.lstrip(" \t")
            sev = "error" if (is_python and (line.startswith(FULLWIDTH_SPACE) or not stripped.startswith("#"))) else "warning"
            issues.append(Issue(path, lineno, "fullwidth-space", "含全角空格 U+3000", sev))
        if is_python and line.startswith("\t"):
            issues.append(Issue(path, lineno, "tab-indent", "Python 用了 Tab 缩进，应改为 4 个空格"))

    if is_python:
        issues.extend(_check_python_identifiers(path, text))
    return issues


def _check_python_identifiers(path: str, text: str) -> list[Issue]:
    issues: list[Issue] = []
    try:
        tokens = tokenize.generate_tokens(io.StringIO(text).readline)
        for tok in tokens:
            if tok.type == tokenize.NAME and not tok.string.isascii():
                issues.append(Issue(path, tok.start[0], "non-ascii-name",
                                    f"标识符含非 ASCII 字符：{tok.string!r}"))
    except (tokenize.TokenError, IndentationError, SyntaxError) as exc:
        issues.append(Issue(path, 1, "tokenize", f"无法解析（可能有语法错误）：{exc}", "warning"))
    return issues


def check_file(path: Path | str) -> list[Issue]:
    """检查单个文件。钩子在每次改完文件后调用它。"""
    p = Path(path)
    if _is_secret_file(p):
        return []
    try:
        raw = p.read_bytes()
    except OSError as exc:
        return [Issue(str(p), 1, "unreadable", f"无法读取：{exc}", "warning")]
    return check_bytes(str(p), raw, p.suffix.lower() in {".py", ".pyi"})


def iter_files(targets: list[str], exts: set[str]) -> list[Path]:
    files: list[Path] = []
    for target in targets:
        p = Path(target)
        if p.is_file():
            files.append(p)
            continue
        if not p.is_dir():
            continue
        for root, dirs, names in os.walk(p):
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in SKIP_DIRS]
            for name in names:
                fp = Path(root) / name
                if fp.suffix.lower() in exts or name.lower() in {".env.example"}:
                    files.append(fp)
    return sorted(set(files))


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
        if callable(reconfigure):
            reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="编码与隐形字符检查（只读）")
    parser.add_argument("targets", nargs="*", default=["."], help="要检查的文件或目录")
    parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="以 JSON 输出")
    parser.add_argument("--errors-only", action="store_true", help="只显示 error 级别")
    parser.add_argument("--ext", action="append", default=[], help="额外检查的扩展名，如 .vue")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    exts = set(DEFAULT_EXTS) | {e if e.startswith(".") else "." + e for e in args.ext}
    files = iter_files(args.targets, exts)
    all_issues: list[Issue] = []
    for fp in files:
        all_issues.extend(check_file(fp))
    if args.errors_only:
        all_issues = [i for i in all_issues if i.severity == "error"]

    errors = sum(1 for i in all_issues if i.severity == "error")
    warnings = len(all_issues) - errors
    if args.json:
        print(json.dumps({"files": len(files), "errors": errors, "warnings": warnings,
                          "issues": [asdict(i) for i in all_issues]}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
    else:
        for i in all_issues:
            tag = "错误" if i.severity == "error" else "提醒"
            print(f"{i.path}:{i.line}: [{tag}:{i.rule}] {i.message}")
        print(f"扫描 {len(files)} 个文件：错误 {errors} 个，提醒 {warnings} 个。")
        if not files:
            print("注意：没有扫描到任何文件，请检查路径是否正确。")
    return 1 if errors else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
''',
    "tools/install.py": r'''"""安装 / 更新 / 卸载 ai-dev-kit（可以反复运行，结果一样）。

做的事：
1. 没有 kit.config.json 时，从示例复制一份，并自动填好当前 Python 路径；
2. 把 skills/ 和 style-packs/ 里的技能同步到 ~/.copilot/skills/（个人技能，所有项目可用）；
3. 把 agents/ 里的自定义代理同步到 ~/.copilot/agents/；
4. 把 instructions/personal.md 合并进 ~/.copilot/copilot-instructions.md（只替换标记之间的内容，你自己写的部分不动）；
5. 写入 ~/.copilot/hooks/ai-dev-kit.json，注册三个钩子：会话开始注入上下文、调用工具前拦截、改完文件检查；
6. 记录安装清单到 ~/.ai-dev-kit/installed.json，卸载和更新时只动自己装的东西；
7. 被覆盖的文件先备份到 ~/.ai-dev-kit/backups/时间戳/。

用法（在工具包根目录）：
    python tools/install.py               # 安装或更新
    python tools/install.py --dry-run     # 只显示将要做什么
    python tools/install.py --no-hooks    # 不注册钩子
    python tools/install.py --hook-style powershell   # 旧版 Copilot 不支持 exec 时用
    python tools/install.py --uninstall   # 卸载
装完重启 Copilot（或在会话里输入 /skills reload），再运行 python tools/kit_doctor.py 检查。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import shutil
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import kitlib

BEGIN = "<!-- ai-dev-kit:begin（这段由 ai-dev-kit 安装脚本维护，请不要手动修改） -->"
END = "<!-- ai-dev-kit:end -->"
HOOK_FILE = "ai-dev-kit.json"
TEXT_SUFFIXES = {".md", ".txt", ".json", ".py", ".yaml", ".yml"}


class Installer:
    def __init__(self, dry: bool):
        self.dry = dry
        self.home = kitlib.copilot_home()
        self.stamp = time.strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
        self.backup_dir = kitlib.DATA_DIR / "backups" / self.stamp
        self.manifest_path = kitlib.DATA_DIR / "installed.json"
        self.manifest: dict[str, Any] = kitlib.load_json(self.manifest_path, {}) or {}
        self.actions: list[str] = []

    def say(self, msg: str) -> None:
        self.actions.append(msg)
        print(("[预演] " if self.dry else "") + msg)

    def backup(self, path: Path) -> None:
        if not path.exists() or self.dry:
            return
        target = self.backup_dir / path.relative_to(self.home) if self.home in path.parents else self.backup_dir / path.name
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        if path.is_dir():
            shutil.copytree(path, target, dirs_exist_ok=True)
        else:
            shutil.copy2(path, target)

    # -------------------------------------------------- 配置

    def ensure_config(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if kitlib.CONFIG_PATH.exists():
            cfg = kitlib.load_config()
            self.say(f"使用已有配置：{kitlib.CONFIG_PATH}")
        else:
            cfg = kitlib.load_json(kitlib.EXAMPLE_CONFIG_PATH, {}) or {}
            cfg["python"] = sys.executable
            self.say(f"创建配置 {kitlib.CONFIG_PATH}（Python 填为 {sys.executable}），请按实际情况修改项目路径和启动脚本。")
            if not self.dry:
                kitlib.save_json(kitlib.CONFIG_PATH, cfg)
        for proj in cfg.get("projects", []):
            if proj.get("path") and not Path(proj["path"]).is_dir():
                print(f"  ! 项目 {proj.get('name')} 的目录不存在：{proj['path']}（不影响安装，记得改 kit.config.json）")
        return cfg

    # -------------------------------------------------- 渲染与同步

    def render(self, text: str, cfg: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        kit = str(kitlib.KIT_ROOT).replace("\\", "/")
        return text.replace("{{KIT_ROOT}}", kit).replace("{{PYTHON}}", kitlib.python_exe(cfg).replace("\\", "/"))

    def copy_tree(self, src: Path, dst: Path, cfg: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        if self.dry:
            return
        if dst.exists():
            shutil.rmtree(dst)
        for path in src.rglob("*"):
            if path.is_dir() or "__pycache__" in path.parts:
                continue
            target = dst / path.relative_to(src)
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            if path.suffix.lower() in TEXT_SUFFIXES:
                kitlib.write_text(target, self.render(kitlib.read_text(path), cfg))
            else:
                shutil.copy2(path, target)

    def sync_skills(self, cfg: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
        sources: list[Path] = []
        for base in (kitlib.KIT_ROOT / "skills", kitlib.KIT_ROOT / "style-packs"):
            if base.is_dir():
                sources.extend(p for p in sorted(base.iterdir()) if p.is_dir() and not p.name.startswith("_")
                               and (p / "SKILL.md").is_file())
        installed: list[str] = []
        skills_home = self.home / "skills"
        previous = set(self.manifest.get("skills", []))
        for src in sources:
            dst = skills_home / src.name
            if dst.exists() and src.name not in previous:
                self.say(f"备份同名的已有技能：{dst}")
                self.backup(dst)
            self.copy_tree(src, dst, cfg)
            installed.append(src.name)
        for stale in sorted(previous - set(installed)):
            target = skills_home / stale
            if target.exists():
                self.say(f"移除已不存在的旧技能：{stale}")
                if not self.dry:
                    shutil.rmtree(target)
        self.say(f"同步技能 {len(installed)} 个到 {skills_home}：{', '.join(installed)}")
        return installed

    def sync_agents(self, cfg: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
        agents_home = self.home / "agents"
        previous = set(self.manifest.get("agents", []))
        installed: list[str] = []
        for src in sorted((kitlib.KIT_ROOT / "agents").glob("*.agent.md")):
            dst = agents_home / src.name
            if dst.exists() and src.name not in previous:
                self.backup(dst)
            if not self.dry:
                kitlib.write_text(dst, self.render(kitlib.read_text(src), cfg))
            installed.append(src.name)
        for stale in sorted(previous - set(installed)):
            target = agents_home / stale
            if target.exists():
                self.say(f"移除已不存在的旧代理：{stale}")
                if not self.dry:
                    target.unlink()
        self.say(f"同步自定义代理 {len(installed)} 个到 {agents_home}：{', '.join(a.replace('.agent.md', '') for a in installed)}")
        return installed

    def merge_instructions(self, cfg: dict[str, Any], remove: bool = False) -> None:
        target = self.home / "copilot-instructions.md"
        current = kitlib.read_text(target) if target.exists() else ""
        if BEGIN in current and END in current:
            head, rest = current.split(BEGIN, 1)
            _, tail = rest.split(END, 1)
            outside = (head.rstrip() + "\n\n" + tail.lstrip()).strip()
        else:
            outside = current.strip()
        if remove:
            new = outside + ("\n" if outside else "")
            self.say(f"从 {target} 移除工具包指令段")
        else:
            block = self.render(kitlib.read_text(kitlib.KIT_ROOT / "instructions" / "personal.md"), cfg).strip()
            new = (outside + "\n\n" if outside else "") + BEGIN + "\n" + block + "\n" + END + "\n"
            self.say(f"合并个人指令到 {target}（你原有的内容保留在标记段之外）")
        if new != current and not self.dry:
            self.backup(target)
            kitlib.write_text(target, new)

    def write_hooks(self, cfg: dict[str, Any], style: str) -> None:
        target = self.home / "hooks" / HOOK_FILE
        py = kitlib.python_exe(cfg)
        hooks_dir = kitlib.KIT_ROOT / "hooks"

        def entry(script: str, timeout: int, matcher: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
            path = str(hooks_dir / script)
            if style == "powershell":
                item: dict[str, Any] = {"type": "command", "powershell": f"& '{py}' '{path}'", "timeoutSec": timeout}
            else:
                item = {"type": "command", "exec": py, "args": [path], "timeoutSec": timeout}
            if matcher:
                item["matcher"] = matcher
            return item

        data = {
            "version": 1,
            "hooks": {
                "sessionStart": [entry("session_start.py", 10)],
                "preToolUse": [entry("guard.py", 5)],
                "postToolUse": [entry("post_edit.py", 10, "edit|create|apply_patch|str_replace_editor|write|str_replace|insert")],
            },
        }
        self.say(f"写入钩子配置 {target}（方式：{style}）")
        if not self.dry:
            self.backup(target)
            kitlib.save_json(target, data)

    def remove_hooks(self) -> None:
        target = self.home / "hooks" / HOOK_FILE
        if target.exists():
            self.say(f"删除钩子配置 {target}")
            if not self.dry:
                self.backup(target)
                target.unlink()

    def save_manifest(self, skills: list[str], agents: list[str], hooks: bool) -> None:
        data = {"kit_root": str(kitlib.KIT_ROOT), "copilot_home": str(self.home), "skills": skills,
                "agents": agents, "hooks": hooks, "installed_at": kitlib.now(),
                "kit_version": (kitlib.load_json(kitlib.KIT_ROOT / "plugin.json", {}) or {}).get("version", "")}
        if not self.dry:
            kitlib.save_json(self.manifest_path, data)

    # -------------------------------------------------- 卸载

    def uninstall(self) -> None:
        for name in self.manifest.get("skills", []):
            target = self.home / "skills" / name
            if target.exists():
                self.say(f"删除技能 {name}")
                if not self.dry:
                    shutil.rmtree(target)
        for name in self.manifest.get("agents", []):
            target = self.home / "agents" / name
            if target.exists():
                self.say(f"删除代理 {name}")
                if not self.dry:
                    target.unlink()
        self.merge_instructions({}, remove=True)
        self.remove_hooks()
        if self.manifest_path.exists() and not self.dry:
            self.manifest_path.unlink()
        self.say("卸载完成。工具包目录、记忆和日志都保留着，需要的话手动删除。")


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    kitlib.setup_console()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="安装 / 更新 / 卸载 ai-dev-kit")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--no-hooks", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--hook-style", choices=["exec", "powershell"], default="exec")
    parser.add_argument("--uninstall", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    if sys.version_info < (3, 9):
        print("需要 Python 3.9 以上。")
        return 2
    inst = Installer(args.dry_run)
    print(f"工具包目录：{kitlib.KIT_ROOT}\nCopilot 配置目录：{inst.home}\n")
    if args.uninstall:
        inst.uninstall()
        return 0
    if not inst.home.exists():
        print(f"提醒：{inst.home} 不存在。如果你从没启动过 Copilot CLI，先启动一次再安装；"
              "如果你用 COPILOT_HOME 指定了别的目录，请先设置好这个环境变量。")
        if not args.dry_run:
            inst.home.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    cfg = inst.ensure_config()
    skills = inst.sync_skills(cfg)
    agents = inst.sync_agents(cfg)
    inst.merge_instructions(cfg)
    if args.no_hooks:
        inst.remove_hooks()
    else:
        inst.write_hooks(cfg, args.hook_style)
    inst.save_manifest(skills, agents, not args.no_hooks)
    if not args.dry_run:
        (kitlib.DATA_DIR / "logs").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    print("\n下一步：")
    print("  1. 按实际情况检查 kit.config.json（项目路径、启动脚本、受保护目录）")
    print("  2. 重启 Copilot CLI；在会话里输入 /skills list 和 /agent，应能看到 kit- 开头的技能和代理")
    print("  3. 运行 python tools/kit_doctor.py 做一次完整体检")
    print("  4. 运行 python tools/selftest.py 确认工具包自身没有问题")
    print("  5. 双击 widget/start_widget.bat 打开桌面挂件")
    if inst.dry:
        print("\n以上为预演，没有改动任何文件。")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
''',
    "tools/scope.py": r'''"""本次修改范围（scope）：把"允许修改的文件名单"变成守卫钩子能强制执行的文件。

写入位置：<项目>/.ai-dev-kit/scope.json，并自动把 .ai-dev-kit/ 加进 .git/info/exclude
（只影响本机，不会被提交）。守卫钩子发现编辑的文件不在名单里，就直接拒绝。

用法：
    python scope.py set --project tap-ai-backend --task "修2：K2a 收尾" \
        --files tests/conftest.py src/tap_ai/application/library_kg.py --globs "tests/unit/test_library_kg*"
    python scope.py add --project tap-ai-backend --files src/tap_ai/api/dto.py
    python scope.py show --project tap-ai-backend
    python scope.py clear --project tap-ai-backend
    python scope.py from-prompt --project tap-ai-backend prompt.txt   # 从提示词的【允许修改的文件】段落读取
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import kitlib


def project_root(name: str) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    """返回 (git 仓库根, 项目根)。两者可能不同，例如 fwd-tap-ingest 在 fwd-tap-common 仓库里。"""
    proj = kitlib.get_project(kitlib.load_config(), name)
    if not proj:
        raise SystemExit(f"kit.config.json 里没有登记项目：{name}")
    return Path(proj.get("git_root") or proj["path"]), Path(proj["path"])


def scope_path(root: Path) -> Path:
    return root / kitlib.SCOPE_DIRNAME / kitlib.SCOPE_FILENAME


def ensure_excluded(git_root: Path) -> None:
    exclude = git_root / ".git" / "info" / "exclude"
    if not exclude.parent.is_dir():
        return
    text = kitlib.read_text(exclude) if exclude.exists() else ""
    if ".ai-dev-kit/" not in text.split():
        kitlib.write_text(exclude, text.rstrip("\n") + ("\n" if text else "") + ".ai-dev-kit/\n")


def _norm_list(items: list[str] | None, base: Path, root: Path) -> list[str]:
    out: list[str] = []
    for item in items or []:
        item = item.strip().strip("`").strip()
        if not item:
            continue
        p = Path(item)
        if p.is_absolute():
            out.append(kitlib.rel_posix(p, root))
        else:
            out.append(kitlib.rel_posix(base / p, root))
    return sorted(set(out))


def load(root: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return kitlib.load_json(scope_path(root), {}) or {}


def cmd_set(args: argparse.Namespace, add: bool = False) -> int:
    git_root, proj_root = project_root(args.project)
    root = proj_root
    data = load(root) if add else {}
    files = set(data.get("files", [])) | set(_norm_list(args.files, proj_root, root))
    globs = set(data.get("globs", [])) | set(args.globs or [])
    data.update({
        "task": args.task or data.get("task", ""),
        "files": sorted(files),
        "globs": sorted(globs),
        "allow_locked": bool(getattr(args, "allow_locked", False) or data.get("allow_locked", False)),
        "updated": kitlib.now(),
    })
    kitlib.save_json(scope_path(root), data)
    ensure_excluded(git_root)
    print(f"已{'追加' if add else '设置'}修改范围：{scope_path(root)}")
    return cmd_show(args)


def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    _, root = project_root(args.project)
    data = load(root)
    if not data:
        print("当前没有设置修改范围（守卫不限制修改哪些文件，只做通用拦截）。")
        return 0
    print(f"任务：{data.get('task', '')}")
    print("允许修改的文件：")
    for f in data.get("files", []):
        exists = (root / f).exists()
        print(f"  - {f}{'' if exists else '（新文件）'}")
    for g in data.get("globs", []):
        print(f"  - {g}（通配）")
    if data.get("allow_locked"):
        print("  ! 允许修改依赖和锁定文件")
    return 0


def cmd_clear(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    _, root = project_root(args.project)
    path = scope_path(root)
    if path.exists():
        path.unlink()
        print("已清除修改范围。")
    else:
        print("本来就没有设置修改范围。")
    return 0


SECTION = re.compile(r"【本次允许修改的文件】(.*?)(?=【|\Z)", re.S)
PATH_IN_LINE = re.compile(r"[\w./\\-]+\.[A-Za-z0-9]+|[\w./\\-]+/\*+[\w.*]*")


def cmd_from_prompt(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    text = kitlib.read_text(args.prompt_file)
    m = SECTION.search(text)
    if not m:
        print("提示词里没有找到【本次允许修改的文件】段落。")
        return 1
    files: list[str] = []
    globs: list[str] = []
    for line in m.group(1).splitlines():
        line = line.strip().lstrip("-*0123456789.、 ").strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("其他文件"):
            continue
        for token in PATH_IN_LINE.findall(line):
            (globs if "*" in token else files).append(token.replace("\\", "/"))
    if not files and not globs:
        print("段落里没有识别出文件路径。")
        return 1
    args.files, args.globs = files, globs
    task = re.search(r"【任务\s*([^】]*)】", text)
    args.task = args.task or (task.group(1).strip() if task else "")
    return cmd_set(args)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    kitlib.setup_console()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="本次修改范围")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    for name in ("set", "add"):
        p = sub.add_parser(name)
        p.add_argument("--project", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--task")
        p.add_argument("--files", nargs="*")
        p.add_argument("--globs", nargs="*")
        p.add_argument("--allow-locked", action="store_true", help="允许修改 pyproject.toml / uv.lock 等")
    for name in ("show", "clear"):
        p = sub.add_parser(name)
        p.add_argument("--project", required=True)
    p = sub.add_parser("from-prompt")
    p.add_argument("--project", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--task")
    p.add_argument("--allow-locked", action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("prompt_file")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.cmd == "set":
        return cmd_set(args)
    if args.cmd == "add":
        return cmd_set(args, add=True)
    if args.cmd == "show":
        return cmd_show(args)
    if args.cmd == "clear":
        return cmd_clear(args)
    return cmd_from_prompt(args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
''',
    "memory/_global/2026-11-02-git-add-named.md": r'''---
id: 2026-11-02-git-add-named
type: convention
project: _global
subject: 提交时逐个点名添加文件，不用 git add . 或 git add -A
tags: git, 提交, 子模块
status: active
priority: high
created: 2026-11-02
verified: 2026-11-18
source: fwd-tap-common 子模块 dirty
citations:
  - user:2026-11-02 git diff --stat 出现 fwd-tap-common | 0，是嵌套子模块有未提交改动
---
事实：仓库里有处于 dirty 状态的嵌套子模块时，git add . 会把子模块指针一起提交。
原因：会把无关改动带进提交，回滚也麻烦。
怎么用：用 commit_helper.py 生成逐个点名的 git add 命令，再用 git diff --cached --stat 核对。
''',
    "memory/_global/2026-11-02-powershell-utf16.md": r'''---
id: 2026-11-02-powershell-utf16
type: lesson
project: _global
subject: Windows PowerShell 5 用 > 或 Out-File 写出的文件是 UTF-16
tags: 编码, PowerShell, UTF-16
status: active
priority: high
created: 2026-11-02
verified: 2026-11-18
source: .env.example 变成 UTF-16 事故
citations:
  - user:2026-11-02 git diff 把 .env.example 显示为 Bin，原因是文件变成了 UTF-16
---
事实：Windows PowerShell 5 的重定向和 Out-File 默认写成 UTF-16 LE，git 会把它当成二进制文件，Python 读取也会出错。
原因：文件编码被悄悄改掉，很难第一时间发现。
怎么用：不用 > / Out-File / Set-Content 写源码或配置；改文件一律用编辑工具；守卫钩子会拦截这些命令。
''',
    "memory/_global/2026-11-01-r3-checklist.md": r'''---
id: 2026-11-01-r3-checklist
type: preference
project: _global
subject: 任务提示词统一用 R3 格式，并以核对表收尾
tags: 提示词, 核对表, 验收
status: active
priority: high
created: 2026-11-01
verified: 2026-11-18
source: 用户多次确认
citations:
  - user:2026-11-01 用户要求提示词使用编号、绝对禁止、贴原始输出和核对表
---
事实：模型经常只做了三件事里的一两件就说完成，所以每个任务都要编号、写明绝对禁止、要求贴原始输出，结尾用核对表逐项给证据。
原因：没有核对表时，漏做的事项很难被发现。
怎么用：完成报告必须包含核对表，每一项写明完成 / 未找到 / 未做 / 已停下，并附 文件:行号 或命令。
''',
    "memory/_global/2026-11-02-self-comparison-test.md": r'''---
id: 2026-11-02-self-comparison-test
type: lesson
project: _global
subject: 对比测试的两边必须相互独立，不能拿模块和它自己比
tags: 测试, 无效测试, 对比
status: active
priority: normal
created: 2026-11-02
verified: 2026-11-18
source: 修1b 新旧白名单对比
citations:
  - user:2026-11-02 sinks._kg_schema 改为从 kg_schema 导入后，“新旧白名单相等”测试变成恒等
---
事实：当被比较的两个对象来自同一处定义时，相等测试永远通过，证明不了任何事情。
原因：这类测试会给出虚假的安全感。
怎么用：要证明“改动前后取值不变”，就拿上一次提交的版本（git show HEAD:路径）和当前版本比较。
''',
    "memory/_global/2026-11-02-zero-tests-is-failure.md": r'''---
id: 2026-11-02-zero-tests-is-failure
type: lesson
project: _global
subject: “0 个测试运行”和“收集阶段中断”都算失败
tags: pytest, 验收, 收集错误
status: active
priority: high
created: 2026-11-02
verified: 2026-11-18
source: 修1b 验收事故
citations:
  - user:2026-11-02 在错误目录跑 pytest 显示 no tests ran；缺 pptx 时 7 个文件收集失败，新测试其实一个都没跑
---
事实：pytest 显示 no tests ran、collected 0 items 或 Interrupted: N errors during collection 时，相关测试根本没执行，不能当作通过。
原因：曾经因此误以为新测试已通过，实际上从未运行。
怎么用：验收时必须贴出工作目录和测试数量；用 run_checks.py，它会自动识别这几种情况。
''',
    "memory/_global/2026-11-02-root-cause-depth.md": r'''---
id: 2026-11-02-root-cause-depth
type: lesson
project: _global
subject: 第一个报错不一定是根因，要一直追到能解释为什么
tags: 调试, 根因, 依赖
status: active
priority: high
created: 2026-11-02
verified: 2026-11-18
source: kg_schema 循环导入事故
citations:
  - user:2026-11-02 “缺 pptx”下面还藏着 kg_schema 与 sinks 的循环导入
---
事实：表面原因（缺少 python-pptx）被修好之后，真正的问题（循环导入）才暴露出来。
原因：只修表面原因会让人以为问题解决了。
怎么用：修 bug 时写出“为什么会这样”的完整链条；修完后用独立进程单独导入每个模块再验证一次。
''',
    "memory/tap-ai-backend/2026-11-02-no-touch-qa-plugin.md": r'''---
id: 2026-11-02-no-touch-qa-plugin
type: decision
project: tap-ai-backend
subject: 问答知识插件属于同事业务，我们的任务不修改它
tags: 边界, 同事, knowledge 插件
status: active
priority: high
created: 2026-11-02
verified: 2026-11-18
source: 用户 11-02 确认

  - src/tap_ai/plugins/extensions/knowledge/settings.py#KnowledgeSettings
---
事实：src/tap_ai/plugins/extensions/knowledge/ 下的 settings、plugin、tools、prompts、spaces 以及 azure_search、neo4j_graph 由同事负责，我们不改。
原因：越线修改会影响同事的问答业务。

    "memory/tap-ai-backend/2026-11-02-jp-poc-readonly.md": r'''---
id: 2026-11-02-jp-poc-readonly
type: decision
project: tap-ai-backend
subject: jp-poc 索引和图谱只读，入库链路一律拒绝写入
tags: jp-poc, 只读, Azure, Neo4j
status: active
priority: high
created: 2026-11-02
verified: 2026-11-18
source: 用户确认的安全约束
citations:
  - user:2026-11-02 jp-poc 只能读，只能用查询密钥；Neo4j 写入只允许导入脚本且限定 scope=jp-poc
  - src/tap_ai/plugins/ingest/sdk_ingest.py#_contains_forbidden_index
---
事实：Azure 的 jp-poc 索引只读；Neo4j 中 scope=jp-poc 的数据只能由导入脚本写入；hk 的 Neo4j 数据现阶段不删不改。
原因：jp-poc 是共享的演示数据，hk 的 Neo4j 与同事共用。
怎么用：任何写入前先确认目标索引和 scope；本地把 TAP_AI_LIBRARY_KB_GRAPH_ENABLED 设为 false。
''',
    "memory/tap-ai-backend/2026-11-02-settings-direct.md": r'''---
id: 2026-11-02-settings-direct
type: convention
project: tap-ai-backend
subject: 配置直接用 settings.字段 访问，不用 getattr 加默认值
tags: 配置, settings, getattr
status: active
priority: normal
created: 2026-11-02
verified: 2026-11-18
source: K2b 精简版
citations:
  - src/tap_ai/config/settings.py#library_kb_graph_enabled
---
事实：getattr(settings, "x", False) 会掩盖字段名拼错、字段被删等问题，并且让默认值分散在各处。
原因：开关和默认值应该只在 settings.py 定义一次。
怎么用：直接写 settings.library_kb_graph_enabled；需要新开关先在 settings.py 定义，并尽量默认开启、少加开关。
''',
    "memory/tap-ai-backend/2026-11-02-api-style.md": r'''---
id: 2026-11-02-api-style
type: convention
project: tap-ai-backend
subject: 接口只用 GET/POST，业务错误返回 HTTP 200 加 code
tags: 接口, 规范, 错误码
status: active
priority: normal
created: 2026-11-02
verified: 2026-11-18
source: 后端接口规范
citations:
  - src/tap_ai/api/library_kb.py#@router.post
---
事实：新接口只用 GET 和 POST；业务错误返回 HTTP 200 加 {code, message}（例如 CCB001）；分页返回 items、total、page、page_size。
原因：前端和已有接口按这个约定处理。
怎么用：写接口前先加载风格包里的“HTTP 接口”卡片。
''',
    "memory/README.md": r'''# 记忆库

一条记忆一个文件，按项目分目录：

- `_global/`：所有项目通用的教训和偏好
- `<项目名>/`：只对这个项目有效（项目名与 kit.config.json 里一致）
- `_archive/`：已过期、已被取代的记忆（不再注入，保留备查）

不要手写文件头，用 `python tools/mem.py add ...` 新增，它会检查长度、重复、密钥，并核对引用。
详细说明见 `docs/05-记忆系统.md`。
''',
    "memory/fwd-tap-ingest/2026-11-02-sdk-not-in-backend-env.md": r'''---
id: 2026-11-02-sdk-not-in-backend-env
type: fact
project: fwd-tap-ingest
subject: 后端 pyproject 没有声明 SDK 依赖，本地需手动安装 SDK 及其解析依赖
tags: 依赖, pptx, 环境
status: active
priority: normal
created: 2026-11-02
verified: 2026-11-18
source: 修1b 排查
citations:
  - pyproject.toml#python-pptx
---
事实：SDK 的 pyproject 把 python-pptx、python-docx、openpyxl、pypdf 列为必需依赖，但后端 pyproject 没有引用 SDK，后端虚拟环境里默认既没有 SDK 也没有这些依赖。
原因：缺依赖时，导入 SDK 的解析器会失败，入库插件可能整个不可用。
怎么用：本地用 uv pip install --offline 手动补装；执行 uv sync 后需要重新安装；线上环境如何安装 SDK 要问同事。
''',
    "memory/fwd-tap-ingest/2026-11-02-kg-schema-leaf.md": r'''---
id: 2026-11-02-kg-schema-leaf
type: lesson
project: fwd-tap-ingest
subject: kg_schema.py 必须是最底层文件，不得导入 tap_ingest_sdk 的模块
tags: 循环导入, kg_schema, SDK
status: active
priority: high
created: 2026-11-02
verified: 2026-11-18
source: 修1b
citations:
  - src/tap_ingest_sdk/kg_schema.py#本文件不得导入
---
事实：kg_schema.py 曾导入 sinks._kg_schema，而后者又反过来导入 kg_schema，先导入 kg_schema 时就会报错。
原因：后端图谱接口直接导入 kg_schema，一定会触发这个问题。
怎么用：kg_schema.py 只能导入标准库；类型常量只在这里定义一次，其他文件从这里导入。
''',
    "widget/start_widget.bat": r'''@echo off
rem Start the ai-dev-kit desktop widget without a console window.

    start "" "%ProgramFiles%\Python312\pythonw.exe" "%WIDGET%"
    goto :eof
)

    start "" pythonw "%WIDGET%"
    goto :eof
